de~Sitter presentation displays at a horn, rather than posited at each point of the bead. \emph{And the presentation that demonstration must be carried in is fixed, because \eqref{eq:shape-invariant} cannot be evaluated on the reassigned chart's surface at all.} The volume of a line times a sphere carries the coordinate extent of the line, so the combination is not scale-free there; and wherever $-f<0$ the surface carries no Riemannian volume, so the combination has no real value---at the comoving turnaround $-f=-1$. \emph{The test is therefore a test of the de~Sitter presentation's layer}, which is where the sphere is displayed and where the continuation is the question. \emph{And carried in that presentation the requirement reduces, because the imaginary segment turns out to be transparent to the test and the flow does not.} The layer at a horn follows from the closed slicing's own equation $a'=a\sqrt{a^2/\alpha^2-1}$ with $a(0)=\alpha$, giving $a(\eta)=\alpha/\cos\eta$, so there the layer is $a^2$ times the unit round $S^3$ and \eqref{eq:shape-invariant} returns its value with the factor cancelling. \emph{Carrying that factor off the positive axis leaves the modulus exactly fixed}---$|\mathcal R V^{2/3}|=43.8232\ldots$ for $a>0$, $a<0$ and $a=\pm i$ alike---\emph{while the principal value picks up a cube root of unity fixed by $\arg a$}, the lap's own thirds. \emph{So the lift is not what the sphere has to survive}: what it has to survive is the shear of the flow that carries it, and the carrying is conformal on the layers only where the constant-$r$ foliation is umbilic. \emph{On \eqref{eq:sds-static} that condition is $rf'=2f$, whose only root is $r=3M$ with the mass carried free}---so it is a statement about the family and not about a member---\emph{and at the Nariai member that radius takes the value $\alpha/\sqrt3$}, the merged horizon's areal radius, with the shear non-vanishing at the comoving turnaround and away from the seam. \emph{The coincidence of those loci is computed and is not an identification of the two objects}: the umbilic radius of the foliation and the merged horizon's areal radius agree in value at the forced member. \emph{And the sheared layer's own invariant settles it, which is why the continuation is stated here as failing rather than as open.} The foliation's trace-free extrinsic curvature is axisymmetric with $\sigma_1=-2\sigma_2$---one distinguished direction against an equal pair, which is the squashing \eqref{eq:shape-invariant} parametrises, so the test is applied in the direction the flow actually shears. \emph{Carrying the horn's round datum along it, the squashing obeys $\dd\ln\varepsilon/\dd r=f'/2f-1/r$, which is identically $\dd\ln(\sqrt f/r)/\dd r$}, so $\varepsilon\propto\sqrt f/r$ with no layer metric written down at any point of the lap. \emph{The response to a squashing has no linear term}---$g(1+u)/g(1)=1-\tfrac89u^2-\tfrac{8}{81}u^3$ exactly, which is the second-order stationarity above in closed form---\emph{so a squashing of either sign lowers the invariant}. \emph{And approaching the seam the squashing runs to zero}: at the Nariai member $f$ has its double root there, so $\varepsilon$ vanishes linearly in $r-r_{N}$ while the parameter along the flow diverges as $-\ln(r-r_{N})$. \emph{So the round shape is not carried to the seam, and the obstruction is in the approach rather than at a point}---the seam lies at infinite parameter and the layer degenerates on the way to it\rcpt{P15_the_sheared_layers_invariant_is_obtained_in_closed_form_and_the_squashing_runs_to_zero_approaching_the_seam_so_the_shape_is_not_carried}. \emph{What the degeneration leaves is not nothing, and saying what it is completes the answer.} A squashing running to zero collapses the Hopf direction at finite curvature: by the Berger expression above $\mathcal R\to$ a finite limit while the volume vanishes linearly in $\varepsilon$, so the $S^3$ degenerates onto its Hopf base rather than shrinking. \emph{The surviving factor is the construction's own angular block $r^2\dd\Omega^2$ at $r=r_{N}=1/\sqrt\Lambda$}---which is the areal radius of the $S^2$ in the merged horizon's near-horizon geometry of Proposition~\ref{prop:throat}. \emph{So the cosmological layer does not carry its sphere through the seam; it becomes the throat's $S^2$ there}, and the Hopf direction it gives up is the one the reassignment of \S\ref{sec:properframe} trades into $\chi$. \emph{And the two descriptions are not merely in agreement at that radius: they are one geometry, which the seam limit of the carried layer gives outright.} Because the $\chi$-block coefficient depends on cosmic time alone, the shift $\dd\chi+\dd\tilde\tau/(-f)$ is an exact differential, so with $\hat\chi=\chi+\int\dd\tilde\tau/(-f)$ and $\dd T=\dd r/\sqrt{-f}$ the four-metric is identically
\begin{equation}
\dd s^2=-\dd T^2+(-f)\,\dd\hat\chi^2+r^2\dd\Omega^2,\qquad (\dd r/\dd T)^2=-f,
\label{eq:layer-proper}
\end{equation}
with $T$ the proper time orthogonal to the cosmological layers. \emph{The lapse diverges exactly where $-f$ vanishes}---which is $|\partial_\chi r|=1$---\emph{so that divergence is the cosmological slicing going null at the seam and belongs to the degeneracy rather than to the chart}: $T$ grows like $\Lambda^{-1/2}\ln[1/(r-r_{N})]$ at the forward seam's double root, while at the back seam's simple zero the same integral converges. \emph{In that parameter the approach is exponential}, $r-r_{N}\propto e^{-\sqrt\Lambda\,T}$, \emph{so the $\hat\chi$ scale factor $\sqrt{-f}$ has rate exactly $\sqrt\Lambda$ and two-dimensional curvature $2\Lambda$---a $\dS_2$ of radius $1/\sqrt\Lambda$}, which is Proposition~\ref{prop:throat}'s own $\sqrt{-2/f''(r_{N})}$ reached from the flow rather than read off the static chart. \emph{The angular block tends to $r_{N}^2\dd\Omega^2$ and both blocks approach their limits linearly in $r-r_{N}$, so the warping switches off and the limit is a direct product}. \emph{The limit metric is Einstein with $R_{ab}=\Lambda g_{ab}$, has $\mathcal R=4\Lambda$ and Kretschmann $8\Lambda^2$---the static metric's own value at $r_{N}$, computed separately}\rcpt{P15_the_seam_limit_of_the_carried_layer_with_its_chi_block_is_prop_throats_ds2_times_s2_obtained_with_both_radii_read_off_the_flow}. \emph{And the join is then an identity rather than a coincidence}: the squashing above is $\sqrt{-f}/r$ and the scale factor here is $\sqrt{-f}$, \emph{so the layer's squashing is the throat's own $\dS_2$ scale factor divided by the areal radius}. The layer's loss of shape and the throat's $\dS_2$ are one object described twice, and the coincidence of radii at the forced mass is not a coincidence\rcpt{P15_the_conjecture_reduces_to_umbilicity_because_a_conformal_carrying_preserves_the_invariants_modulus_across_the_lift_and_the_foliation_is_umbilic_only_at_the_seam}. \emph{Two things about the lap are settled without it.} \emph{The angular block enters only through $r^2$ and is blind to the sign of $r$, and the $\chi$ block is not}: $-f$ carries $2M/r$, which is odd, so two chart values of equal $|r|$ carry $S^2$ factors of equal radius and $\chi$ blocks that differ---at $|r|=A$ they are opposite in sign. \emph{So $|r|$ is the label of the layer's size and not of the layer}, and the identification that closes the lap is the translation $r\mapsto r+\sqrt3\alpha$ carrying one seam to the other, $-2\alpha/\sqrt3+\sqrt3\alpha=+\alpha/\sqrt3$ exactly, rather than the reflection $|r|$ would implement. \emph{And $r=0$ is not a single event but one on each worldline}, since $\partial_\chi r=\partial_\tau r$ makes the level sets of $r$ the $45^\circ$ lines of constant $\tau+\chi$; the vanishing volume and divergent curvature of the layer at that one cosmic time are the areal coordinate degenerating, which this paper's own reading of the branch point already states. \emph{The cosmological seam is likewise a locus and not a point, and its position is fixed exactly}: the handover from deceleration to acceleration is the inflection of $r(\tilde\tau)$, which vanishes where $\cosh(3\tilde\tau/\alpha)=2$, and the areal radius there is $2^{-1/3}A=\alpha/\sqrt3=r_N$ identically---\emph{so the cosmological seam is the merged horizon of Proposition~\ref{prop:throat}}, where $f$ and $f'$ vanish together, and the reassigned layer's $\chi$ block $-f=(\partial_\chi r)^2-1$ vanishes there with them, so that layer is null at the handover. \emph{On the signed chart it is null at both of the lap's unit-speed loci}---the two roots of \eqref{eq:chi-block-factored}---\emph{and the translation that closes the lap carries one to the other}, so the two are one locus of the substrate\rcpt{P15_the_layers_three_metric_obtained_by_restriction_is_timelike_through_the_whole_interior_of_the_lap_so_the_continuation_does_not_go_through_and_the_invariant_cannot_adjudicate_it}. A literal closed-Friedmann reading would put $\Omega_k=-\Omega_\Lambda\approx-0.685$ into the distance relation and be excluded; CR avoids that not by tuning but because its distance slicing is the flat one, the curvature living on the orthogonal layers. Spatial curvature and dark energy are one $\Lambda$ read on two slicings---the corpus identity, here at the observational rung. \emph{And that reading is an identity rather than a correspondence, which is worth having explicitly because it also separates two lengths the same sentence could be read as conflating.} The curvature radius the closed readout returns is
\begin{equation}
\frac{c}{H_0\sqrt{\Omega_\Lambda}}=\frac{c}{H_0}\sqrt{1+\frac{2}{x_0^{3}}}=\alpha=\sqrt{3/\Lambda},
\label{eq:closed-readout-radius}
\end{equation}
exact in the construction's own parameterisation---symbolic residual zero, and $2\times10^{-16}$ on both backgrounds---\emph{so what $|\Omega_k|=\Omega_\Lambda$ fixes is the substrate's throat.} \emph{That is not the layer's present radius}: $\alpha/r_0=\sqrt3/x_0=1.0320$, so the two differ by $3.2$ per cent, and the quantity the low-multipole floor is built on is $r_0$ (\S\ref{sec:largescale}, below) while the quantity the closed readout names is $\alpha$. \emph{The content the identity carries is unchanged and is if anything firmer}---$\Omega_k$ is not a free parameter, because its magnitude is forced to $\Omega_\Lambda$ by one $\Lambda$---\emph{and what the two lengths say is that the readout and the layer are the same $\Lambda$ read at two epochs of the bead rather than one number serving twice}\rcpt{P15_the_construction_fixes_no_closed_layer_radius_because_the_nariai_mass_closes_the_route_that_fixes_it_in_de_sitter_and_the_one_radius_it_does_fix_is_alpha}.

\paragraph{The low-multipole floor.}
The closed $S^3$ carries a discrete mode spectrum, labelled by integer degree $L$, with comoving wavenumber $k_L=\sqrt{L(L+2)}/r_0$ and the lowest physical mode the quadrupole $L=2$ (the monopole $L=0$ is the background, the dipole $L=1$ pure gauge). \emph{The eigenvalue in that wavenumber is the scalar one, and the dipole's exclusion is derived rather than conventional}: the traceless second covariant derivative of a degree-one harmonic is the \emph{zero map}, rank $0$ where the full Hessian at that degree has rank $2$, so it vanishes identically and not for want of modes to act on---and the same map has full rank $L+1$ from $L=2$ up. \emph{The companion's tensor tower floors at the same degree for the opposite reason}, its transverse-traceless eigenvalue being $L(L+2)-2$, which has no member at either of the two lowest degrees~\cite{JanzenCosmogenesis}: \emph{so one tower excludes two degrees it has and the other has none to exclude, and the shared number carries two statements}\rcpt{P15_the_dipole_parenthesis_is_right_on_its_own_tower_and_does_not_reach_the_other_one_because_the_scalar_floor_is_a_vanishing_traceless_hessian_and_the_tensor_floor_is_an_empty_degree}. The decoupling fixes how these project to the sky, and the fixing is the whole point. Because the distance slicing is flat (Proposition~\ref{prop:flat}), the photons are projected through the \emph{flat} geometry---the comoving angular-diameter distance is $D_M=D_C$, the same flat-$\Lambda$CDM observable that places the acoustic scale at $\ell_A\approx301$---while only the \emph{source} modes carry the closed-$S^3$ quantization. The transfer is therefore the discrete closed-$S^3$ spectrum projected through the flat spherical Bessel $j_\ell(k_L D_C)$, \emph{not} the hyperspherical transfer of a literal closed universe: that transfer carries the closed \emph{distance} relation, which CR does not have, and would (wrongly, here) deliver the lowest mode to the quadrupole and no deficit. The flat projection places the discrete modes at
\begin{equation}
\ell_L\simeq\sqrt{L(L+2)}\;\frac{D_C}{r_0},
\label{eq:lowell}
\end{equation}
with $r_0$ the present $S^3$ areal radius and $D_C$ the comoving distance to last scattering, both fixed without new parameters: $D_C\approx1.4011\times10^{4}\ \mathrm{Mpc}$ is the comoving distance to last scattering and $r_0\approx5051\ \mathrm{Mpc}$ is the Nariai amplitude $2^{1/3}/\sqrt\Lambda$ carried to the present epoch $u=\operatorname{arcsinh}\sqrt{\Omega_\Lambda/\Omega_m}\approx1.22$, both on the background the distance data fix. The stretch factor $D_C/r_0\approx2.774$---the ratio of the flat projection distance to the curvature radius, the decoupling made numerical---carries the lowest mode to $\ell_2\approx7.8$, with no source below it.

\emph{What \eqref{eq:lowell} returns is the peak argument of a projection and not a multipole}, and the distinction is load-bearing wherever a statement about the degree $L$ is carried to the sky. The weight a layer degree places on a multipole is $w_\ell(L)=(2\ell+1)j_\ell(\ell_L)^2$, a genuine distribution over $\ell$ by the Bessel closure $\sum_\ell(2\ell+1)j_\ell(x)^2=1$; evaluated on the two lengths above, \emph{the printed value sits near the ninetieth percentile of that distribution rather than at its centre}---$P(\ell\le\ell_L)$ running $0.875$ to $0.961$ over $L=1$ to $8$, with $\ell_L$ above the modal multipole by between $1.74$ and $3.02$, a displacement of order one mode that is bounded both ways and does not grow with degree. \emph{And the width crosses the spacing between consecutive degrees at $L=3$}: against a mean spacing of $2.19$ to $2.39$, the standard deviation runs $1.20$, $1.83$, $2.45$, $3.07$, $3.70$, $4.32$, $4.94$, $5.56$ for $L=1$ to $8$, and each degree's band of $2\%$ weight overlaps its neighbour's in $4$ to $7$ multipoles from the third degree up. \emph{So the map is quasi-injective for the first two degrees and smears without bound above them}, and a statement exponential in $L$ transfers to $\ell$ as a statement about the lowest contributing degree rather than keeping its form---the bottom edge of each band, not its centre, because a steep envelope is dominated there. \emph{The figures above are the geometric minimum}: they are the projection kernel alone, with no transfer function, window or visibility in them, so a real projection can only be wider. \emph{What this does not disturb is the floor itself}: the leak below $\ell_2$ falls fast with degree, $P(\ell\le3)$ running $0.591$, $0.151$, $0.059$, $0.032$ for $L=1$ to $4$, so the floor is softened by about a sixth of one mode and not erased\rcpt{P15_no_paper_fixes_the_primordial_normalisation_because_three_sentences_inherit_it_and_the_L_to_ell_map_is_a_projection_whose_width_passes_the_mode_spacing}\rcpt{P15_the_projection_join_inverts_the_expected_picture_because_the_odd_degree_lands_lower_than_the_even_floor_and_arrives_less_suppressed_so_the_lowest_multipoles_draw_on_the_degree_the_bound_would_have_removed}.

\begin{figure}[t]
\centering

With $D_C\approx1.4011\times10^{4}$~Mpc and $r_0\approx5051$~Mpc fixed parameter-free by $\Lambda$, the stretch $D_C/r_0\approx2.774$ carries the lowest mode to $\ell_2\approx7.8$, with no source below it. The full flat-projection transfer turns this into a low-multipole power deficit ($\ell\lesssim8$); on a genuine Boltzmann transfer (the late integrated Sachs--Wolfe sourced above the floor and so retained by the discrete source) it sits at $0.487$, $0.435$, $0.359$, $0.666$ of the flat-$\Lambda$CDM expectation at $\ell=2,3,4,5$, recovering by $\ell\approx8$---\emph{a deficit whose minimum falls at $\ell=4$}, so that the quadrupole and octopole are its shoulders and not its floor. Those figures are read through the exact transfer, and the photon hierarchy built for this programme (\S\ref{sec:instrument}), carried to the present, agrees with them to three per cent at every multipole (\S\ref{sec:scope}): \emph{the shape and the depth are both cross-validated}. This is a mild deficit, non-discriminating against the observed large-angle power within the lowest multipoles' cosmic variance (shaded). \emph{What the branch-point filter does to this prediction at these multipoles---and what it does not do---is taken up in the text below rather than here.}}

The full flat-projection transfer confirms the estimate and sharpens it into a prediction. Carrying the discrete spectrum through $j_\ell(k_L D_C)$ with the scale-invariant measure---the first-principles closed-$S^3$ Harrison--Zel'dovich weight~\cite{Harrison1970,Zeldovich1972} $w_L=(L+1)/(L(L+2))$, fixed as the mode degeneracy $\beta^2$ (with $\beta=L+1$) times the per-mode power $1/(\beta(\beta^2-1))$ that equal power per logarithmic interval in $k$ requires, and reducing to the continuum $dk/k$ at large $L$ so that $\ell(\ell+1)C_\ell$ returns to the flat plateau as $D_C/r_0\to0$ (receipt: \texttt{verify\_lowell\_exact\_measure.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_lowell_exact_measure})---yields a low-multipole \emph{deficit}: $\ell(\ell+1)C_\ell$ falls below its high-$\ell$ plateau for $\ell\lesssim7$ and recovers by $\ell\approx8$, because the flat projection of a discrete source with a hard lowest mode at $k_2$ leaves the lowest multipoles starved (receipts: \texttt{verify\_closedS3\_nonsync.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_closedS3_nonsync}). 

The one effect that partly fills it is the late integrated Sachs--Wolfe term~\cite{SachsWolfe1967}, sourced near the observer where small line-of-sight distances send wavenumbers $k\gtrsim k_2$---\emph{above} the floor---down to $\ell\sim2$. 

By the decomposition of the empirical-forcing paper~\cite{JanzenModernParallax} the cumulative term is the standard Sachs--Wolfe and integrated Sachs--Wolfe---CR's own differential-expansion floor being zero under uniform expansion---so this late boost rides the same modes the discrete source keeps, and the deficit is milder than the bare Sachs--Wolfe starvation alone. 

The depth is a matter for a \emph{genuine Boltzmann transfer} rather than for the geometry, \emph{and the reason is that the deficit is two effects and not one}. In the pure Sachs--Wolfe limit the suppression splits exactly into a \emph{floor}---the projection integral truncated below $k_2$---and a \emph{ladder coarseness}, the discrete sum minus that same truncated integral. \emph{At $\ell=5$ and $6$ the two are comparable and pull opposite ways}: the ladder puts back power a pure cut-off removes. \emph{And they respond to the fitted parameter with opposite signs}---under $\pm2\%$ in $r_{0}$ the floor moves by a few per cent while the full result moves by tens---so the location is the floor and is robust for that reason, and the depth is the ladder and is volatile for that reason\rcpt{H1_the_low_multipole_deficit_is_two_effects_and_the_ladder_imprint_dies_where_the_transfer_is_narrow}. \emph{The recovery multipole is the ladder's too}: with it the spectrum recovers by $\ell\approx8$, on the floor alone not until $\ell\approx10$. Because the only CR modifications are the discrete source and the flat projection---every transfer ingredient (Sachs--Wolfe, early and late integrated Sachs--Wolfe, Doppler) shared with flat-$\Lambda$CDM~\cite{JanzenModernParallax}---the exact CMB temperature transfer $\Delta_\ell(k)$ of a standard Boltzmann code \emph{is} CR's transfer, and the discrete spectrum is read off it: $C_\ell^{\mathrm{CR}}=\sum_{L\ge2}w_L\,\Delta_\ell(k_L)^2$ against the continuum $\int d\ln k\,\Delta_\ell(k)^2$, the measure $w_L$ being exactly $d\ln k_L/d L$ so the CR sum is the $L{=}2$-floored Riemann sum of the very integral $\Lambda$CDM does continuously. 

Carried out with the exact transfer (gate-validated: the continuum reproduces the code's own $C_\ell$ to four figures; and the $r_0$ sensitivity is measured rather than assumed: the \emph{shape} is stable---the minimum stays at $\ell=4$ and the recovery multipole moves by $0.1\%$---while the \emph{depths} drift by up to $15\%$ at $\ell=4$ under $\pm2\%$ in $r_0$), the deficit is \emph{milder} than a Sachs--Wolfe-only estimate suggests, and it has a shape: $\ell(\ell+1)C_\ell$ sits at $\approx0.487$, $0.435$, $0.359$ and $0.666$ of the flat-$\Lambda$CDM expectation at $\ell=2,3,4,5$, recovering by $\ell\approx8$ (receipts: \texttt{verify\_lowell\_boltzmann.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_lowell_boltzmann} for the transfer, and the depths with the late integrated Sachs--Wolfe term carried to the present\rcpt{P15_the_low_multipole_depth_gap_closes_and_two_defects_were_cancelling}). \emph{The prediction is therefore not a monotone recovery from the quadrupole but a dip whose minimum falls at $\ell=4$}\rcpt{P15_the_low_ell_minimum_is_at_ell_four}---and the quadrupole and octopole, the two multipoles a large-angle discussion reaches for first, are its two \emph{shallowest} points. 

The shape is not one code's, and that was checked here by running it rather than by comparing tables\rcpt{P15_the_second_arm_actually_run}. 

Driving the photon hierarchy built for this programme (\S\ref{sec:instrument}) through the same discrete projection puts the minimum at the same multipole, $\ell=4$, and recovers at the same place, $\ell\approx7$--$8$. \emph{The two independent Boltzmann treatments cross-validate the depth as well as the shape} once each carries the late integrated Sachs--Wolfe term to the present: on the exact transfer the deficit is $0.487$, $0.435$, $0.359$, $0.666$ of the flat expectation at $\ell=2$ to $5$, and the hierarchy agrees to three per cent at every multipole and one per cent above $\ell=5$ (\S\ref{sec:scope})\rcpt{P15_the_low_multipole_depth_gap_closes_and_two_defects_were_cancelling}\rcpt{P15_the_lowell_sweep_is_reproduced_by_a_producer_in_the_repository_and_the_bank_is_that_producers_output_rounded}. 

The reason is the late integrated Sachs--Wolfe: it is sourced near the observer at wavenumbers $k\gtrsim k_2$ (small line-of-sight distances), \emph{above} the floor, so the discrete source \emph{retains} it rather than losing it, and the deficit is milder than a Sachs--Wolfe-only treatment of the same source would give. 

This is a parameter-free deficit, set by $\Lambda$ through $r_0$ and $D_C$, and what is robust is its existence, its location ($\ell\lesssim8$, geometric, set by $k_2 D_C$), its minimum at $\ell=4$, and that the single floor $k_2$ starves $\ell=2$ and $\ell=3$ \emph{together}: the exact discrete measure and the Doppler term (super-horizon velocity through $j_\ell'$) each leave the $\ell=2$-to-$\ell=3$ ratio near unity, if anything suppressing the octopole slightly more (receipts: \texttt{verify\_lowell\_exact\_measure.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_lowell_exact_measure}, \texttt{verify\_doppler\_lowell.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_doppler_lowell}). Whether this predicted deficit matches the observed sky is the data test, against the large cosmic variance of the lowest multipoles. Confronted with the measured low multipoles, the verdict is a \emph{wash}. CR predicts a smooth modest deficit bottoming at $\ell=4$; the observed sky has a sharp quadrupole-only dip ($\sim0.2$) with a near-$\Lambda$CDM octopole ($\sim0.6$--$1.0$), so CR sits \emph{above} the observed quadrupole and \emph{below} the observed octopole, matching neither sharply. Under the exact cosmic-variance likelihood the net over $2\le\ell\le10$ is $\Delta(-2\ln L)\approx+1.6$ (range $+0.2$ to $+3.4$ across the octopole estimator), a small residual well inside cosmic variance (receipts: \texttt{confront\_lowell\_data.py}\rcpt{P15_confront_lowell_data}\rcpt{P15_the_low_multipole_depth_gap_closes_and_two_defects_were_cancelling}). \emph{Broken up by multipole, the sector's weight does not lie where its name suggests}: the quadrupole \emph{rewards} this cosmology ($-2.5$, its milder dip nearer the observed low power than flat $\Lambda$CDM's), the octopole penalises it at $+1.0$, and the largest single-multipole penalty is the $+2.0$ at $\ell=4$---the prediction's own minimum, and the one multipole of the four that the large-angle literature does not treat as anomalous. The low multipoles are thus a genuine but \emph{non-discriminating} sector: neither a parameter-free correspondence success nor a sharp falsification edge, but a mild deficit consistent with $\Lambda$CDM within the lowest multipoles' cosmic variance.

\emph{One further question belongs with this prediction, and it is a located one.}
The branch-point filter that sets what crosses into the expansion leg is, in its quantum reading, a Euclidean
kernel whose action on a mode is controlled by an adiabaticity parameter $C/\mu_{n}$, with $\mu_{n}$ the
harmonic index of the layer's $S^{3}$ tower~\cite{JanzenCanonicalTime}. That parameter is small for all but the
lowest harmonics and reaches order unity only at $n=2$ and $n=3$, where it takes the values $0.70$ and $0.47$.
\emph{Three independent routes put the approximations at their limit on the same scale}: the transmission
calculation's boundary at $\ell\approx2.5$, the projection's incompleteness below
$k\sim2\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ ($\ell\approx3$), and this parameter's reaching order unity at $n=2,3$.
Whether that is a loss of control is decided by computing the filter rather than estimating it.


\emph{The transmitted amplitude at low multipoles can be computed rather than estimated, and doing so settles
what the estimates left open.} The branch-point filter's action on a mode is, in the quantum reading, a
Euclidean kernel~\cite{JanzenCanonicalTime}. The exponential-of-an-integral form $e^{-\int\omega\,\dd\eta}$ is
a WKB approximation whose adiabaticity parameter is of order unity at $\ell\lesssim5$; but the underlying mode
equation $\dd^{2}\psi/\dd\eta^{2}=\omega^{2}\psi$, with $\omega=kc_{s}$ and $\dd\eta=\dd s/a$ along the
segment, is well posed at every $k$, and integrating it is a matter of numerics rather than of approximation.
Composing exact constant-$\omega$ transfer matrices in the stable direction over the segment
($\lvert\Delta\eta\rvert=3.33874$ in units $\alpha=1$) gives exact/WKB ratios of $0.926$, $0.913$, $0.901$,
$0.891$ and $0.889$ at $\ell=2,3,5,15,40$: \emph{the WKB form is accurate to $7$--$11\%$ at every multipole
from $2$ to $40$}, with the exact transmission slightly smaller, so the filter is marginally stronger than the
approximation
gives\rcpt{P15_the_exact_transmission_ratios_are_recomputed_and_the_offset_saturates}.


\emph{Two readings suggested by the adiabaticity parameter alone are thereby excluded.} The first is that
the treatment is uncontrolled at $\ell=2$--$5$: it is not, and the parameter was the wrong figure of merit,
measuring as it does the fractional error in an exponent that is itself small at low $\ell$. \emph{The residual's own behaviour settles this independently of any argument about the parameter}: the ratio \emph{decreases} monotonically with $\ell$---$0.926$, $0.913$, $0.901$, $0.891$, $0.889$---so the discrepancy is smallest at $\ell=2$, where the adiabaticity parameter is of order unity, and largest at $\ell=40$, where it is small. An error of adiabatic origin runs the other way, WKB being an expansion \emph{in} that parameter, so such an error is largest where the parameter is $O(1)$ and vanishes as it does. \emph{What the numbers show is therefore a systematic offset rather than an adiabatic breakdown}---the WKB form is uniformly slightly generous and the exact filter uniformly slightly stronger, which is a correction of known sign and bounded size and not a loss of control\rcpt{P15_the_exact_transmission_ratios_are_recomputed_and_the_offset_saturates}\ldg{spectral_theory}. The second concerns the comparison with the observed sky. The correction is very nearly $\ell$-independent---varying by
$4\%$ across a factor of twenty in $\ell$---so between $\ell=2$ and $\ell=3$ it alters their ratio by $1.4\%$.
\emph{The structural expectation that a single floor starves $\ell=2$ and $\ell=3$ together is therefore not
relieved by the exact treatment}, and the observed sky's selective suppression of $\ell=2$ alone, by a factor
of order three, remains unaccounted for. The low-multipole prediction of this section therefore stands as stated, and so does the discrepancy it faces.

\emph{Finally, the two Boltzmann treatments above agree}, on the depth as well as on the shape, the minimum and the recovery, once both carry the late integrated Sachs--Wolfe term to the present (\S\ref{sec:scope}). What both supersede is the Sachs--Wolfe-analytic estimate ($0.22$ and $0.20$ at $\ell=2,3$), which takes the Sachs--Wolfe source as the matter-domination value $\tfrac13\Phi$, whereas at recombination $\rho_r/\rho_m=0.317$ and the source is $0.4006\,\Psi$---a $20\%$ error in the very ratio of Sachs--Wolfe to integrated Sachs--Wolfe that fixes how much the latter fills the bare deficit.


\section{What the progenitor supplies: the transmission proof}\label{sec:transmission}

The decomposition's decisive proof is here: \emph{why} the substrate transmits the spectral shape rather than imprinting one of its own. It turns on how a mode approaches the locus it meets. Two horizon cases bound the question: a non-degenerate horizon, and the degenerate \emph{Nariai double root}, at $r=+\alpha/\sqrt3$---the front seam, where $f$ and $f'$ vanish together so that $\kappa=0$. \emph{The locus the modes cross is neither}: the branch point sits at $r=0$, carries no surface gravity because $f$ diverges there rather than vanishing, and is not a Killing horizon at all. \emph{And the lap's other seam is a case of its own, which must be kept apart from the double root rather than folded into it.} The back seam at $r=-2\alpha/\sqrt3$ is one point of the substrate with the front seam, met a lap earlier, but the two chart values carry different orders of contact with zero: the back seam is the horizon cubic's \emph{simple} root, so $f'$ does not vanish there and $\kappa=3\sqrt3/4\alpha$. It is the lap's one non-degenerate horizon. \emph{Whose horizon it is depends on the level, and the framework states both}: at substrate level it is the merged horizon re-entered, one point with the front seam; read locally on the sheet the collapse runs on, it presents as the cosmological horizon of the mass, with the radius timelike outside it and the geometry static within~\cite{JanzenCRframework,JanzenSlicing}. What matters for the dichotomy is neither of those but the order of the root, and that is unambiguous. Both cases of the dichotomy are therefore realised on the lap, and which one a given seam realises is a question about that seam and not about the pair.

\begin{proposition}[Transmission dichotomy]\label{prop:transmission}
For a mode approaching a horizon where the metric function behaves as $f\sim(r-r_h)^p$, the tortoise coordinate $r_*=\int dr/f$ controls the approach. At a non-degenerate horizon ($p=1$, $f\sim2\kappa(r-r_h)$, surface gravity $\kappa>0$) the integral is logarithmic and the approach exponential, $(r-r_h)\sim e^{2\kappa r_*}$. At the degenerate Nariai double root ($p=2$, $f\sim-\Lambda(r-r_{N})^2$, $\kappa=0$) the integral is $r_*\sim+1/[\Lambda(r-r_{N})]$ and the approach power-law, $(r-r_{N})\sim+1/(\Lambda r_*)$.
\end{proposition}

\noindent\emph{Argument.} The two tortoise integrals directly (receipts: \texttt{verify\_geometry.py}, anchor~4\rcpt{P15_verify_geometry}). $\qquad\square$

\begin{remark}[The leg does not tilt it either]\label{rem:transmission-leg}
The proposition shows that a degenerate Killing horizon carries no scale, so it cannot imprint one, and Proposition~\ref{prop:transmit} shows the same of the branch point. The
collapse leg supplies the other half of the same conclusion, by an independent route. Horizon entry on that leg
occurs at $x=k\eta/\sqrt3=1/\sqrt3$ \emph{for every $k$}---the radiation era's own scale invariance, which
\S\ref{sec:envelope} recovers from the substrate geometry---so every mode leaves the leg carrying the same
free-oscillation amplitude $\Psi(1/\sqrt3)/2=0.4835\,\Psi_i$, a single $k$-independent
number\rcpt{AS_amplitude_leftward}. \emph{So the leg multiplies the spectrum by a constant and the branch point
imprints nothing}: neither carries a scale, and a spectrum can only be tilted by something that does. The tilt
observed is therefore the progenitor's, established from both sides of the branch point rather than one.
\emph{And the list of places a tilt could have come from is closed rather than merely exhausted.} A tilt is
$\dd\ln P/\dd\ln k$; written as this construction writes its output the spectrum is a product of three
factors---the measure, the interior's multiplier, and the progenitor's amplitude---so the log-derivative is a
sum and the candidates are its terms. \emph{The measure's term is the constant $3$ and cannot tilt anything},
which leaves exactly two: the interior's and the progenitor's. \emph{Both are accounted for above}, the
interior's being the constant this leg multiplies by and the progenitor's being the inherited datum, and a third
term would have to be an action that is neither the interior's nor the progenitor's and is not the
measure\rcpt{P15_the_enumeration_derived_from_the_construction_has_two_members_and_the_rows_own_identity_is_one_of_them_with_the_other_held_constant}.

\emph{``Carries a scale'' has an operational form, and it is worth recording because it
is what makes the dichotomy testable rather than descriptive}: a kernel carries a scale exactly when
the tilt one fits to it \emph{depends on which band one fits}. Fitted on four disjoint bands, the
degenerate ($p=2$) kernel returns one tilt to sixteen decimal places while the non-degenerate ($p=1$)
kernel spreads by several units, and that spread shrinks as $\kappa$ grows---a larger scale pushing
its knee past the range. Restoring a scale to the degenerate kernel by hand destroys the agreement, so
the scale-freedom is doing the work and not the
arithmetic\rcpt{N8_the_transmission_dichotomy_is_a_capacity_dichotomy}\ldg{information_theory}.
\emph{This restates Proposition~\ref{prop:transmission} and proves nothing further}; what it adds is
a test one can run on a kernel without knowing where it came from.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[What the branch point does not fix]\label{rem:branchpoint-not-a-condition}
Both halves of the dichotomy are statements about the spectrum's \emph{shape}: a degenerate horizon carries no
scale, and neither does horizon entry on the leg, so neither can tilt what passes. \emph{Neither is a pointwise
condition on the perturbation state at the branch point}, and the distinction matters for anyone computing the
handover. The single free function per mode is the freedom of the \emph{spectrum}---the progenitor's amplitude
and tilt---not a claim that the branch point fixes the state component by component. The state itself is whatever the
leg's evolution produces, and \S\ref{sec:envelope} supplies it in closed form: the potential from the leg's own
equation, the effective temperature oscillating freely from horizon entry, and the density contrast as their
difference.

The Hamiltonian constraint is not an additional condition to impose there. In this framework it \emph{is} the
anchor---the map from a cut to its stress-energy, whose energy sector reads the Friedmann equation off the
geometry~\cite{JanzenOperator,JanzenRange}. It is a definition, not a restriction: on the leftward reading the
rate is set by the substrate and the content is read from it, so the perturbed constraint likewise \emph{defines}
the density contrast a given geometry carries rather than restricting which geometries are allowed. Imposing it
at the branch point alongside the leg's own solution therefore over-determines the handover, and the same geometric
state answering to different stress-energy on the two sides is not a failure of matching but the branch point doing
what the level assignment says it does.
\end{remark}

\begin{remark}[What the branch point's degeneracy does not settle]\label{rem:phase-open}
It is tempting to read Proposition~\ref{prop:transmission} as settling the phase as well as the shape: the
tortoise coordinate diverges at the degenerate root, so a mode's accumulated phase would be unbounded and
nothing finite could be inherited. \emph{That reading does not survive its own arithmetic.} The divergence is
geometric and carries no $k$, so an unbounded $kr_{*}$ is a divergent term \emph{linear} in $k$; a linear phase
renormalizes the sound horizon rather than shifting the comb, and an unbounded one would send the comb spacing
to zero and erase the acoustic peaks altogether---excluded by observation, not supported by it. And the modes
never meet the divergence in any case: they cross the branch point at finite conformal time, whereas $r_{*}$ diverges
along a static slicing's approach to the horizon, which is not the collapse trajectory.

What the leg does contribute is finite and, as it happens, invisible in the peak positions. Horizon entry at
$x=1/\sqrt3$ for every $k$ makes the accumulated leg phase $k\eta_{\rm bp}/\sqrt3-1/\sqrt3$: linear in $k$ with
a mode-independent offset. So carrying it across rescales the effective sound horizon by
$1+\eta_{\rm bp}/(\sqrt3\,r_{s})$ and adds a constant, and changes the comb in no other way. \emph{The comb
therefore cannot distinguish a transmitted phase from a reset one}, because a rescaling of $r_{s}$ is absorbed by the angle it is read against. Any discriminant must be a quantity that does not take the same factor---the diffusion scale is one, since $r_{D}$ does not---and that is the discriminant of principle. \emph{The comparison is made, and it does not require $r_{D}$.} The leg's accumulated conformal time is fixed by the geometry rather than fitted: the collapse branch $r=-(2M\alpha^{2})^{1/3}\cosh^{2/3}(3\,\mathrm{Re}\,\tilde\tau/2\alpha)$ has $\cosh\ge1$, so $|r|\ge(2M\alpha^{2})^{1/3}\simeq3792$ Mpc and the branch point is reached only across the lift, along which $\mathrm{Re}\,\tilde\tau$ does not advance and no conformal time accumulates. Hence $\eta_{\rm bp}\approx5\times10^{3}$--$10^{4}$ Mpc, and $r_{s}^{\rm eff}=r_{s}+\eta_{\rm bp}/\sqrt3$ is \emph{dominated} by the leg term---$3.0$--$5.9\times10^{3}$ Mpc against $r_{s}=144$ Mpc. The absorption that takes a small rescaling cannot take this one: reproducing the measured $\ell_{A}=301.8$ would require $D\approx2.9$--$5.7\times10^{5}$ Mpc against the actual $1.4\times10^{4}$ Mpc, a factor of $21$--$41$. \emph{The acoustic phase is therefore reset at the branch point and not carried across it}---established on the peak positions alone\rcpt{BRANCHPT_transmission_character}. The result is robust to the open account of the lift: any conformal contribution the lift is found to carry only adds to an already-excluded total.
\end{remark}

The amplitude separates from the tilt in the same statement. What the progenitor supplies is the potential
power $P_{\Psi}$; what the construction contributes is the factor $(0.4835)^{2}=0.2338$ carrying it to the
observed $A_{s}$. That factor is computed rather than fitted: the inherited quantity is $P_{\Psi}$ and the map from it to
$A_{s}$ is derived, so what the construction takes from the progenitor here is the potential power and
nothing about the map~\cite{JanzenCRframework}.

\begin{figure}[t]
\centering
\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{transmission-dichotomy.png}
\caption{The transmission dichotomy (Proposition~\ref{prop:transmission}). \emph{Left}: the approach to the horizon in the tortoise coordinate $r_*=\int dr/f$, on a logarithmic vertical axis. At a non-degenerate horizon ($\kappa>0$) the approach is exponential, $(r-r_h)\sim e^{2\kappa r_*}$ (a straight line); at the Nariai double root ($\kappa=0$) it is power-law, $(r-r_{N})\sim 1/|r_*|$. \emph{Right}: the logarithmic approach rate $d\ln(r-r_h)/dr_*$ \emph{is} the local scale. The non-degenerate horizon carries a fixed thermal scale $2\kappa$---the mechanism by which a de~Sitter horizon imprints a scale-invariant spectrum ($n_s\to1$); at the Nariai double root the rate falls to zero, carrying \emph{no} scale. The branch point, where the modes cross, is a third case: $f$ diverges there, the tortoise coordinate is finite, and the crossing accumulates no phase at all (Proposition~\ref{prop:transmit}).}
\label{fig:transmission}
\end{figure}

The physical reading is the result (Fig.~\ref{fig:transmission}). The exponential, non-degenerate approach is the thermal (Hawking) law---the mechanism by which a de Sitter horizon \emph{imprints} a scale-invariant spectrum ($n_s\to1$): the exponential redshift erases the infaller's spectral information and stamps the horizon's own thermal scale. The power-law, degenerate approach carries no thermal scale; it is scale-free, and the infalling spectrum is transmitted faithfully. CR's transmission locus is the branch point $r=0$, where $f$ \emph{diverges} as $-2M/r$ so the tortoise coordinate is \emph{finite}---the crossing accumulates no divergent phase and stamps no scale, a third case beyond the dichotomy's two\rcpt{BRANCHPT_transmission_character}. The Nariai double root (Proposition~\ref{prop:throat}, $f''(r_{N})=-2\Lambda$, $\kappa=0$) is \emph{the front seam}, $r=+\alpha/\sqrt3$. It is one point of the substrate with the back seam the bead meets on the way in, the two chart values $r=-2\alpha/\sqrt3$ and $r=+\alpha/\sqrt3$ being the same $\varphi$ modulo $2\pi$ in the phase $\varphi=2\pi r/\sqrt3\alpha$; \emph{being one substrate point does not make them one case of the dichotomy}, since the degeneracy is a property of the root and the back seam's root is simple. The branch point sits two thirds of the lap in from the front seam ($240^\circ$, with $120^\circ$ remaining), so the crossing at $r=0$ and either seam are distinct loci on the lap without a seam being an endpoint of anything. Hence:

\begin{proposition}[The branch point transmits]\label{prop:transmit}
The cosmogenesis branch point is a faithful scale-free transmitter---not because it \emph{is} the degenerate Nariai horizon, which it is not (that is the front seam, at $r=+\alpha/\sqrt3$), but because $f$ there \emph{diverges} as $-2M/r$, so $r_{*}$ is finite and the crossing accumulates no divergent phase at all: nothing about the branch point imprints a scale on what crosses it, and the amplitude $A_s$ and tilt $n_s$ are the progenitor collapse's, not the branch point's. \emph{What a finite tortoise length rules out is a divergent phase, and the crossing does carry a real factor, which is stated here with its size because it is exact.} \emph{The era in which a mode's spectrum is fixed is the one in which it is super-horizon}, and that era is located on this construction by one scalar along the bead: $a''/a$ in conformal time, whose single constant is the pure number $2/3c_0^{2}=2^{-1/3}$ on all three legs, falling monotonically to it at the comoving turnaround and attained again as a \emph{minimum} on the expansion leg at $x=\tfrac12\ln2$ exactly---the midpoint of that leg measured from the branch point, by the $1:\sqrt3:2$ closure of \S\ref{sec:scope} and so at $1.9276\alpha$ rather than at the front seam's $2.3759\alpha$\rcpt{P07_the_back_seam_is_the_laps_one_non_degenerate_horizon_and_its_surface_gravity_is_the_beads_own_acceleration_there}. \emph{So away from the three divergences the only super-horizon harmonic is the monopole}: the horizon degree is $L_{\rm hor}=-1+\sqrt{1+2^{-1/3}}=0.339$, with $L=1$ already sub-horizon by a factor $3.78$ and $L=2$ by $10.08$, so no convention shift of order unity in $k^2$ reaches that gap. \emph{The sky's first acoustic peak, carried to the layer by \eqref{eq:lowell}, has its spectrum fixed within the first per cent of the collapse leg, measured from that leg's far-past end}---so the whole of the lift lies between that locus and the front seam, and the envelope of \S\ref{sec:throat} applies to what the progenitor supplies. \emph{The inheritance is therefore exact for the isotropic mode and for no other}: $T(0)=1$ identically, the monopole being super-horizon everywhere, while every anisotropic harmonic is damped by the parameter-free factor the throat supplies. \emph{Which is the same statement as the isotropizing throat read from the other end}, and it places the origin of the anisotropic spectrum on the expansion leg, where it requires a source the single Nariai worldline of this construction does not carry---named here as owed and developed in neither this paper nor the bead\rcpt{P15_the_progenitor_spectrum_is_defined_before_the_lift_so_the_envelope_applies_to_it_and_the_horizon_scale_on_both_lorentzian_legs_is_the_same_pure_number}. \emph{Equivalently, CR has no inflationary scale-invariant attractor---and the reason is not that the lap carries no non-degenerate horizon, because it carries exactly one.} The three loci fail the imprinting condition for three different reasons, and the reasons are not interchangeable. At the branch point $f$ diverges, so $r_{*}$ is finite and no divergent phase accumulates. At the front seam the root is double, so $\kappa=0$ and the tortoise approach is power-law, carrying no scale by Proposition~\ref{prop:transmission}. At the back seam neither holds: the root is \emph{simple}, $\kappa=3\sqrt3/4\alpha$, and in the tortoise coordinate the approach \emph{is} exponential at rate $2\kappa$---the imprinting law itself. \emph{What fails there is the approach and not the scale, and the reason is characteristic structure rather than a choice of slicing.} The seam is a root of $f$, and the principal symbol of the radial mode equation restricted to $\dd r$ \emph{is} $f$---so every root of $f$ is a characteristic surface of that equation, and the two characteristic speeds $\dd r/\dd\tau=v\pm1$ there are exactly $2$ and exactly $0$. The unbounded approach belongs to the skimming family, the one with speed zero, which fails to arrive at rate $\kappa$ forever; the trajectory that carries the modes rides the other, crossing at speed $2$ and reaching the comoving turnaround $0.878\alpha$ of its own proper time later. \emph{Both rates are real and neither is an artefact}: $r_{*}$ runs at $2\kappa$, proper time along the skimming rays at $\kappa$, and the bead runs on neither. \emph{The thermal factor is in the mode equation---exactly once, and on the branch the modes are not on.} The indicial equation at the seam returns the two exponents $0$ and $-i\omega/\kappa$; they differ by a non-integer for every real $\omega>0$, so the monodromy is diagonal, with no logarithm and no mixing: unity on the regular branch and $e^{-2\pi\omega/\kappa}$ on the skimming one, the Boltzmann factor at $T_{H}=\kappa/2\pi$. \emph{The crossing therefore takes the regular branch, whose transfer across a window of width $d$ tends to unity like $d$}---measured over four decades of $d$, four decades of $\omega/\kappa$ and $\ell=0,2,10$, against a $d$-independent $e^{\pi\omega/\kappa}$ that reaches $10^{136}$ at $\omega=100\kappa$. \emph{So the crossing's own factor is the identity}: not small and not $k$-independent-and-nonzero but exactly $1$, which is neither an amplitude in $A_{s}$ nor a tilt in $n_{s}$, and $2\kappa$ cannot enter through it. \emph{And the one wavenumber a thermal factor could have carried is named rather than left open}: the only scale at the crossing is $\kappa$, so $k_{\rm th}=a_{h}\kappa=3/2$ exactly and $\alpha$-free, which in the convention $k^{2}=L(L+2)$ is $L=0.803$---\emph{below the dipole}, the smallest harmonic the sky carries sitting a factor $2/\sqrt3$ above it, and through \eqref{eq:lowell} acting at $\ell=4.16$ against the first acoustic peak's $220.60$. That factor of $53$ in wavenumber is the counterpart of the $53$ in position: the locus where the sky's first peak has its spectrum fixed lies in the first per cent of the collapse leg from that leg's far-past end while the back seam lies at $46.5$ per cent of the same leg, \emph{so the lap's one non-degenerate horizon stands some fifty times downstream of where the progenitor's spectrum is set, in scale as well as in place, and alters nothing crossing it}\rcpt{P07_the_back_seam_is_the_laps_one_non_degenerate_horizon_and_its_surface_gravity_is_the_beads_own_acceleration_there}\rcpt{P07_the_crossing_rides_the_characteristic_that_crosses_and_the_thermal_factor_rides_the_one_that_does_not_so_two_kappa_cannot_enter_and_the_only_k_it_could_have_carried_is_three_halves}. \emph{What is not computed here is the flux the seam radiates into the lap's future}, which is a question about the quantum state on this background rather than about a classical mode's transfer. \emph{And it is not computable on this construction at all, for two reasons that are properties of the lap rather than of the progenitor.} First, the closure that makes the two seams one locus---the translation $r\mapsto r+\sqrt3\alpha$, which carries $-2\alpha/\sqrt3$ to $+\alpha/\sqrt3$ identically---makes that one locus carry \emph{two} surface gravities, $0$ and $3\sqrt3/4\alpha$, and no $\alpha$ reconciles them; a thermal state is a periodicity at a horizon of a given $\kappa$, and there is no single $\kappa$ here for a period to be $2\pi/\kappa$ of. Second, and independently, the Euclidean section the lift supplies is measured in the bead's own conformal time while the periodicity would be imposed in the seam's Killing time, and the conversion between them, $f/r$, is not constant along the lift and \emph{vanishes at both seams}---the only loci at which such a condition is imposed. \emph{So the construction selects no state}, and the absence is of the object a flux calculation would be about rather than of a result it would return\rcpt{P15_the_seam_flux_is_not_computable_here_because_the_closure_makes_one_locus_carry_two_surface_gravities_and_the_euclidean_section_closes_in_a_clock_whose_conversion_vanishes_at_both_seams}.
\end{proposition}

The absence of any scale where the modes cross is thus the proven reason CR carries the primordial tilt rather than manufacturing it. What the transmitter carries is, from the Schwarzschild--de Sitter side, open: $n_s$ and $A_s$ are inherited, fixed by the progenitor collapse---the same handover that fixes $\rho_r/\rho_m$ and the light-element abundances---and not derived here. \emph{Naming them together is convenient and has concealed that they are not the same kind of quantity}\rcpt{P15_hbar_survives_in_As_and_cancels_in_ns}. The vacuum normalisation is the only place a quantum constant enters the spectrum, so $P(k)=\hbar A_0k^{n_s-1}$ and $\hbar$ is an overall multiplicative factor: \emph{it survives in the amplitude and cancels in every logarithmic derivative}. An amplitude is the spectrum at one wavenumber; a tilt is its ratio to itself at two. \emph{So the construction's inability to force a dimensionless magnitude bears on $A_s$ and not on $n_s$}---the first is permanently inherited, the second need not be. \emph{And read backwards the same statement is useful rather than limiting}: a quantity fixed by the moduli rather than the invariants is one the observation \emph{measures} rather than one the construction must predict, so the observed $A_s$ is a measurement of the progenitor's boundary data. \emph{It does not isolate the mass}: the parent's growing-mode amplitude $D_k$ enters alongside it, which is the corollary recorded above, so the mass must be reached by a route that does not run through the amplitude and checked against the independent requirement that it lie below the Nariai value. \emph{And it is worth saying precisely what that openness is, because a count settles its kind.}\rcpt{P15_the_geometry_transmits_no_parameters} The progenitor family described here is parametrised by $M$ alone---$\alpha$ is the one invariant and $E=1$ a choice of geodesic---so the geometry is one-dimensional; and the Kretschmann along the bead is mass-free in the faller's own proper time, so that one parameter does not survive the approach either. \emph{A quantity identical for every member carries no information about which member it was}, and the collapse geometry therefore transmits not one parameter but none. Against that stand at least four independent inherited numbers. \emph{So `inherited from the progenitor collapse' cannot mean inherited from the collapse geometry}: what is inherited is inherited from the progenitor's matter content---its composition, its entropy per baryon, its perturbation spectrum---and none of that is carried by $M$, the exterior here being exactly vacuum and the marginally-bound interior dust without composition. \emph{The frontier is accordingly not a derivation awaiting a cleverer argument but a modelling task awaiting a progenitor interior}, and what the geometry supplies in its place is a cutoff rather than a content: the segment's conformal length $\lvert\Delta\eta\rvert=3.33874\alpha$ is fixed by $\Lambda$ alone \emph{on the Nariai member}---below it $|\Delta\eta|$ runs as $M^{-1/3}$, reaching $4.21\alpha$ at $M/2$ and $15.50\alpha$ at $M/100$---and says which modes survive without saying how large they were\rcpt{P15_the_exact_transmission_ratios_are_recomputed_and_the_offset_saturates}\rcpt{P15_the_segments_conformal_length_is_the_closed_form_of_the_papers_own_integral_and_the_two_damping_rates_differ_by_the_sound_speed}.
\emph{And the multiplicative statement above settles the one join the inherited pair needed}: the handover's own factor is a constant of exactly the kind named there, so it survives in $A_s$ and cancels in the tilt---the latter identically, with the power to which the transfer enters left free, so no convention choice touches it. \emph{What it costs is a number rather than a mechanism}: the factor is $0.4835^{2}=0.2338$ on the spectrum, $6.49$ per cent against the handover's own three, while a transfer carrying the plasma's sound speed instead shifts the spectrum by $2c_sk\lvert\Delta\eta\rvert$---some $7.6\times10^{3}$ times the tilt budget the refit leaves (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound})---so the sound-speed reading cannot deliver the tilt it would be invoked to carry\rcpt{P15_the_route_from_the_handover_amplitude_to_the_spectral_parameters_is_already_in_print_as_the_hbar_sentence_so_the_tilt_passes_exactly_and_horn_two_cannot_carry_it_at_all}.

\section{Predictions, and the progenitor boundary}\label{sec:predictions}

The signatures, with their maturity marked:
\begin{enumerate}
\item Classical, non-vacuum primordial statistics; no inflationary consistency relation; no substrate-sourced primordial $B$-modes (established, \S\ref{sec:amplitude}).
\item The transmission character: the branch point carries the progenitor tilt and does not drive $n_s\to1$ by any \emph{inflationary} attractor (established, \S\ref{sec:transmission}). \emph{Nor does the construction lack a scale-invariant attractor---it has one, and it is not the inflationary root.}\rcpt{P15_the_collapse_leg_is_scale_invariant} Scale invariance for a background $a\propto(-\eta)^{\beta}$ with $z\propto a$ requires $\beta(\beta-1)=2$, a quadratic with \emph{two} roots: $\beta=-1$, de~Sitter inflation, and $\beta=+2$, a matter-dominated contraction. \emph{The collapse leg of this construction is the second root exactly}: near the branch point $f\to-2M/r$, so the leg is pressureless there and the banked law $r^{3}=\tfrac92M\tau^{2}$ with $a=\lvert r\rvert$ gives $a\propto\tau^{2/3}\propto\eta^{2}$; the mode equation integrated from vacuum data returns a flat spectrum to a part in $10^{4}$. \emph{The mode-selection question that shadows this in the literature was accordingly put to the construction directly, and it answers in the negative.}\rcpt{P15_the_continuation_is_diagonal} 

The scale-invariant spectrum sits in the mode that \emph{dominates} the contraction, whose curvature perturbation goes as $\eta^{-3}$; the mode that survives on the expanding side is the constant one, and from vacuum data its spectrum is $n_s-1=6$. 

Whether the first reaches the second is a connection problem at the branch point, and here it is settled rather than assumed: the exponents $2$ and $-1$ differ by an integer, so a logarithm could mix them, but the $\sinh^{2/3}$ law is \emph{even} in $\tau$, which in conformal time places its correction at $\eta^{6}$ and steps the Frobenius recursion in sixes past a resonance sitting at three. \emph{No logarithm is generated, the continuation acts diagonally, and the scale-invariant spectrum does not reach the observer}---confirmed independently by continuing the dominant solution numerically around the branch point, where the admixture it produces is what the claim turns on, where the admixture falls as $\delta^{2/3}$ and the diagonal entry goes to unity. \emph{What survives is therefore narrower than it first appeared and sharper than what it replaced}: the construction \emph{has} a scale-invariant background, the spectrum that background freezes is confined to the contracting side, and the obstruction is a parity property of the construction's own solution rather than a missing mechanism. \emph{Mixing would require a background correction odd in $\tau$, and that question is settled here too, in the negative.}\rcpt{P15_the_evenness_is_the_time_symmetry} 

Writing the law as $r=Au^{2/3}g(u)$ with $g=[\sinh u/u]^{2/3}$ places every branched factor in $u^{2/3}$ and leaves $g$ even, analytic and non-vanishing at the origin---exactly, since $\log(\sinh u/u)$ is analytic in $u^{2}$ there and the exponential of a series in $u^{2}$ is one. The three sheets differ by a constant cube root of unity, and a constant multiplier cannot change a parity; the conjugate branch is even as well, and carries no branch point at all. \emph{So the evenness is unavoidable, and what it says is not a technical fact but a symmetry}: $r(-\tau)=r(\tau)$, the cosmogenesis is time-symmetric about the branch point, and a time-symmetric background cannot mix a growing mode with a decaying one, since mixing would distinguish a direction of time the background does not. \emph{The same invariance appears one observable over}: the driven acoustic amplitude of \S\ref{sec:coherence} is a resonant Fourier magnitude exactly invariant under time reversal, a theorem about the driving, though what it carries about the peak heights is what that section's instrument does not return. \emph{One symmetry, read at two places.} \emph{And it locates rather than forecloses what would be needed}: this leg is exactly even because it is the marginally-bound geodesic of an exactly vacuum exterior, so a progenitor carrying real matter content---pressure, dissipation, anything with an arrow---would not be, which returns the question to what the progenitor was.
\item Flat distances ($\Omega_k\simeq0$) coexisting with a closed-$S^3$ low-multipole \emph{deficit} below $\ell\approx8$, set parameter-free by $\Lambda$---mild, and on a genuine Boltzmann transfer a dip bottoming at $\ell=4$ ($\approx0.49$, $0.44$, $0.36$, $0.67$ of the flat-$\Lambda$CDM expectation at $\ell=2,3,4,5$), its shape and depth cross-validated between two independent transfers (\emph{established} for the existence, the location, the minimum and the depth (\S\ref{sec:scope}); \emph{non-discriminating} as a test, the deficit sitting inside the largest multipoles' cosmic variance). Confronted with data it is non-discriminating: it starves $\ell=2$ and $\ell=3$ together, matching neither the sharp observed quadrupole-only dip nor the near-$\Lambda$CDM octopole, and sits consistent with $\Lambda$CDM within the lowest multipoles' cosmic variance (\S\ref{sec:largescale}).
\item Coherent acoustic peaks from geometric (null-boundary) phase-fixing rather than super-horizon freeze-out (argued, \S\ref{sec:coherence}).
\item The acoustic scale is resolved in the cosmological development above (\S\ref{sec:flatlcdm}), and the geometric rate~\eqref{eq:native-rate} is the standing CR-versus-$\Lambda$CDM discriminator on data in hand (\emph{established}, \S\ref{sec:flatlcdm})---a strength established before the spectrum, not this section's to re-assert.
\end{enumerate}

The boundary is principled. The substrate carries everything the branch point's null geometry lets it carry---coherence, isotropization, the discreteness floor, the classical character, the faithful transmission---and the scale-free crossing proves precisely where it stops: the spectrum's amplitude and tilt are not the substrate's to set. This is the shape inflation has when it parametrizes its observables through an unknown inflaton potential; CR routes them through an unknown progenitor collapse, with the arrival constrained---whatever the collapse supplies must reach the branch point coherent, isotropized, scale-freely transmitted, and classical.

The symmetry between the two unknown suppliers is only apparent, and the asymmetry is the substance. Inflation is not structure the standard cosmology already contains; it is an added sector---a scalar field with a freely specified potential---introduced to produce features that cosmology was known to lack: the causal contact the horizon problem denies, the flatness the dynamics do not single out, and, once these were in view, a near-scale-invariant spectrum. The potential is then chosen to deliver the observed amplitude and tilt. A model built to be tunable, and tuned to a target known in advance to require tuning, earns precisely what such a model can earn---it establishes that the features are \emph{possible}, that an apparatus permitting them exists. It does not establish that they were \emph{required}, and the routine elevation of the spectral fit into a vindication of inflation conflates the two: that a freely chosen potential can be set to match $n_s$ and $A_s$ is not evidence that inflation occurred, but the statement that a model with enough adjustable structure to fit has been fit. This is the distinction between a world that \emph{requires} a phenomenon and one that merely \emph{permits} it through adjustable parameters---the same distinction the programme draws in reading the cosmic foliation as forced rather than chosen~\cite{JanzenModernParallax}---and it places the inflationary spectrum on the permitting side.

The present cosmology adds no such apparatus. Causal contact, isotropy, flatness, phase coherence, and scale-free transmission are not produced by a field introduced to produce them; they are read off structure the construction already contains---the de~Sitter throat that isotropizes by the no-hair theorem (\S\ref{sec:throat}), the null boundary on which coherence \emph{is} the characteristic-data condition (\S\ref{sec:coherence}), the exactly Euclidean constant-time slice (\S\ref{sec:flatlcdm}), and the degenerate horizon whose vanishing surface gravity transmits rather than imprints (\S\ref{sec:transmission})---each fixed by $\Lambda$ alone, with no parameter free to do the producing. The one quantity it does not derive, the amplitude and tilt, it inherits in exactly the sense flat $\Lambda$CDM inherits the baryon-to-photon ratio; on that single datum the two frameworks stand level, and on everything the inflationary sector is invoked to supply they do not. The verdict this licenses is not symmetric, and it does not await a future measurement. On the data in hand the two align; on the structure each must assume in order to align, one introduces an entire tunable sector and tunes it while the other introduces none---and makes, in its place, falsifiable commitments where inflation keeps its freedom: no scale-invariant attractor \emph{reaching the observer}, no consistency relation, no substrate-sourced $B$-modes (\S\ref{sec:amplitude},~\S\ref{sec:transmission})\rcpt{P15_the_continuation_is_diagonal}. By the economy of assumption that distinguishes a required structure from a permitted one, that comparison is settled by what each theory must presuppose, and it is settled in this cosmology's favour. The bound on that verdict is stated in the same breath: it is a verdict on what each theory must presuppose, and the data axis is split. The expansion history fits the distance ladder and fixes $H_0$ with the acoustic angle (\S\ref{sec:tensions}); the abundances are confirmed within $1\sigma$~\cite{JanzenCosmogenesis}; the spectrum's content remains inherited; and the acoustic peak structure, computed from that background with nothing fitted to it, reproduces the comb to a part in six hundred and is still rejected on $TT$ shape (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound})---the sharpest datum against the construction.

\subsection{The acoustic instrument: what it is validated against, and to what accuracy}\label{sec:instrument}
The low-multipole prediction above and the ratio statements below are carried by a Boltzmann transfer built for this programme, and its standing should be stated plainly rather than assumed. It is a full photon hierarchy with polarisation, second-order tight coupling, massless neutrinos, and a Peebles recombination history; the line-of-sight source carries the monopole with the potential, the Doppler term, the integrated Sachs--Wolfe term, and the quadrupole with its own projection kernel $(j_\ell+3j_\ell'')$.

Against a like-for-like reference---an independent Boltzmann code run at identical parameters, unlensed and with reionisation off---it reproduces the \emph{acoustic peak and trough positions to $\le0.5\%$} across $P_1$--$P_4$, the \emph{ionisation history $x_e(z)$ and its derivative to $\sim\!1\%$} through the visibility peak, matter--radiation equality to $0.02\%$, and the \emph{transfer function to better than $1\%$ for $k<0.02\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$}. Above that wavenumber it agrees to $1$--$2\%$, with the residual scatter in both directions and no trend across the range\rcpt{BUILD_camb_store}.

\emph{Two construction details are worth stating, because both are easy to omit and both have signatures that read as physics.}

\emph{The first is the reach of the mode ladder.} The angular spectrum at multipole $\ell$ is an integral over wavenumber, and the projection kernel $j_\ell(k(\eta_0-\eta))$ has substantial support \emph{above} $k=\ell/D_M$, not merely at it; a ladder cut at $k_{\max}$ therefore truncates the integral, and does so more severely as $\ell$ rises toward $k_{\max}D_M$. Quantitatively, truncating an exact transfer at $k_{\max}D_M=900$ retains $99.2\%$ of $C_\ell$ at $\ell=200$ but only $61\%$ at $\ell=800$ and nothing at all at $\ell=1150$; carrying the ladder to $k_{\max}\ge2\,\ell_{\max}/D_M$ recovers every multipole to better than half a per cent. \emph{The tell is unmistakable once known}: the highest peak simply fails to appear, so a spectrum that should show four acoustic peaks shows three, and the peaks that do appear are displaced downward by an amount that grows with $\ell$. \emph{Increasing the ladder's density at fixed reach does not help}---the loss is the range, not the sampling, which is worth checking explicitly because the two are easily confused.

\emph{The second is the seeding of the conformal time.} It is a cumulative integral, $\eta(a)=\int_{0}^{a}\dd a'/(a'^{2}H)$, and a numerical grid necessarily begins at some finite $a_{\min}$; the interval already elapsed below that point must be carried as a seed rather than set to zero. In radiation domination $H\propto a^{-2}$ makes the integrand constant, so the omitted piece is $a_{\min}/(H_{0}\sqrt{\Omega_{r}})$ exactly---for $a_{\min}=10^{-5}$ it is $4.6\,\mathrm{Mpc}$. \emph{It is a fixed offset at every epoch}, and therefore $0.03\%$ of $\eta_{0}$ but $12\%$ of $\eta$ at $z=10^{4}$: the acoustic scale, the peak positions and the peak ratios are insensitive to it, while any early-time quantity or any comparison against a second code is not. Its signature is instructive---because the potential decays more steeply at larger $k$, a fixed error in the time coordinate reads out as a \emph{smooth, monotone, $k$-growing} error in amplitude, which is the shape one would otherwise attribute to a physical suppression. The grid must also extend below the intended starting redshift, so that the initial epoch is interpolated rather than clamped.

\emph{That residual does not enter the claims made here.} The Cosmological Relativity and flat-$\Lambda$CDM spectra are computed through the \emph{same} machinery, so an instrumental transfer error afflicts both arms identically and cancels in their ratio---and every prediction stated in this paper is a ratio. The low-multipole deficit in particular lives at $\ell\lesssim8$, i.e.\ $k\ll0.02\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$, where the two agree to better than $1\%$. What the residual does forbid is an \emph{absolute} amplitude claim: this instrument should not be read as reproducing the observed small-angle power to better than a few per cent, and no such claim is made.

\section{The open frontier: the pieces this cosmology is built to close}\label{sec:scope}

The result stands on what the substrate determines. What it does \emph{not} yet claim has been carried to a sharp, gradable edge rather than left vague---and those edges are the most interesting part of the programme from here, each a gap to be closed, not papered over. 

We hold them to the criterion of necessity the programme's epistemology names---that a structure is credited for \emph{requiring} a phenomenon, not for \emph{permitting} a value that fits it~\cite{JanzenShadowExistence}---and sort them by it: a gap that is \emph{buildable} is a debt owed, to be built before the claim it bears on is a proof rather than a coherent proposition, while a genuinely external unknown may stay open at no cost, exactly as flat $\Lambda$CDM leaves its own inherited data open. 

The buildable ones below are named as debts, not softened into frontiers. \emph{Of the four, three are run and one is a frontier}: the end-to-end branch-point-to-recombination transfer, the large-angle depth and the likelihood are built and confronted below, what the transfer leaves being a difference in the acoustic contrast rather than in the phase (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound}); and the derivation of the progenitor spectrum is the genuine frontier rather than a debt. Four stand out:
\begin{enumerate}
\item the sufficiency of the null-boundary characteristic data for the observed acoustic coherence (\S\ref{sec:coherence}), argued from the structure of the characteristic initial-value problem, with the time-reversal driving equality established there as a Fourier theorem and the handover at the branch point fixing the phase and the amplitude the datum leaves open. \emph{The end-to-end branch-point-to-recombination transfer is run} (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound}): a two-arm line-of-sight Boltzmann integral, $\Delta_\ell(k)=\int S\,j_\ell(k(\eta_0-\eta))\,d\eta$, carried on this cosmology's own background with the perturbations on the leaf congruence the framework assigns them, and validated on its control to $0.23\%$ in the first-to-second height ratio in the configuration the arm's own numbers are taken in\rcpt{P15_the_third_peak_deficit_is_radiation_driving_and_both_routes_to_the_equality_agree}\rcpt{C59_the_control_reproduces_camb_and_the_height_defect_was_k_truncation}. On this arm it returns a comb of $298.0$ against the sky's $298.4$ and height ratios $2.264$ and $2.298$ against $2.217$ and $2.277$, with the likelihood still rejecting this arm's spectrum on shape; the diffusion-scale signature it carries is located by the joint fit as a Gaussian residual in $\ell$ that no tilt removes (\S\ref{sec:coherence}). \emph{What the transfer leaves is about one multipole at each peak, and the sky cannot resolve it}: the comb is right to a part in six hundred, the phase intercept sits inside the sky's own locating spread, and the fourth peak is $0.9$ to $1.3$ standard deviations out with most of that offset shared with the control. \emph{What remains is not a position at all}: the difference between this arm and the control is a \emph{contrast} about the acoustic envelope---not a phase and not an envelope---it carries three quarters of the likelihood's excess, and its channel is the balance between the monopole and the Doppler terms of the source, bracketed with opposite signs on both instrument paths, and the difference itself is made \emph{between wavenumber and multipole}---in wavenumber the two arms' relative oscillations agree to the measuring statistic's own floor---and not by any one source term nor by the interference between two, the projection raising this arm's contrast on nearly everything it projects and increasingly with wavenumber, the mechanism being the kernel's own acceptance in wavenumber and stated as a law with nothing fitted in it (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound}). \emph{Two things about that excess are measured rather than left to the frontier}: the Doppler channel's coupling to the contrast is negative at every band on the arm's own spectrum and the share of the excess that channel carries is a minority on either envelope, growing with wavenumber and sign-indefinite at the lowest bands; and \emph{the excess's own structure across wavenumber is a step at its lowest band rather than a trend}, the bands above it fitted by a constant, so the discriminating power of every shape comparison across wavenumber sits where the excess carries least structure. \emph{The step survives three running envelopes differing in kind and sits at the second acoustic peak to about a per cent}---sharper than the window that resolves it---\emph{with the lowest band running between a third and two thirds of the rest on every statistic that resolves it}. \emph{Every candidate this comparison holds is evaluable against that step, and between them they carry a minority of it}: the window weighting steps the wrong way, the term mix the right way, the pair as actually composed about two fifths, and the projection width and the Doppler none. \emph{And the step is very largely shared}---both arms step upward at the lowest band and this arm's is shallower by about a tenth and no more, \emph{so the feature belongs to the acoustic physics the arms have in common and the excess's step is the part that fails to cancel}. \emph{That part survives an estimator that is differential by construction}, the two spectra divided before any width is taken so the shared step is never formed---negative on all four bases, both pairings and both aggregations---\emph{and it is an amplitude difference and not a phase one}, the split being exact rather than fitted, so the first acoustic cycle is weakened in this cosmology rather than displaced in it. \emph{The shared part reduces to no single term of the source}, the removal losses summing to nearly twice the whole, though a step-weighted reading of the decomposition is led by the Doppler where an amplitude-weighted one is led by the monopole. \emph{And scored on the differential estimator the candidate list accounts for none of the step}: one channel steps the opposite way to the arm and the other two are not resolved there, where against the mostly-shared quantity they reached two thirds and two fifths---\emph{and they cannot be resolved by the banded family of statistics}, the smallest decidable share standing at three fifths of the step against carried shares of about a third and a thirtieth, with every route to a lower floor taken and the phase-insensitive aggregation lowering scatter and signal by the same factor. \emph{But that limit belongs to the family and not to this comparison}: the likelihood these spectra are confronted with bins at a thirty-fourth of a comb period rather than banding at seven tenths of one, and in its metric the two channels separate from each other at some twenty-five standard deviations, with every candidate departing the arm's way at that band. \emph{What that metric measures is what the data can distinguish, and the data's own rejection of this arm lies elsewhere}: the lowest band carries under one per cent of the excess in fit and changes sign as smooth shape is absorbed, while four bands in the upper half carry some six sevenths of it on an exact decomposition---\emph{so the excess's one prominent feature across wavenumber sits where the rejection is cheapest, and the rejection sits where no smooth shape finds anything}. \emph{But the rejection there is not a level}: bin by bin it is modulated at the comb's own period, reached by none of twenty thousand pooled draws of the instrument's own covariance through the same statistic, with five sixths of it carried by the term that couples the two arms' difference to the control's residual---\emph{so the rejection carries acoustic structure}, the period fixed to the range's own resolution and not finer. \emph{And no banded reading can find it, which is a limit of representation and not attenuation}: one number per band at seven tenths of a period spacing has a Nyquist period of twice that, so eight of the eleven instruments this comparison has used cannot represent a comb-period feature at all, where the likelihood bins at a Nyquist period a twentieth of it and passes it whole. \emph{And across those bands one candidate channel costs the arm's way}: the term mix delivers about four thirds of the arm's cost with the two patterns correlating at seven tenths and nearly half of it misplaced, where the window weighting's cost is \emph{anti}-correlated and the pair as composed matches worse than the term mix alone---\emph{so a total is not a match, and on this metric the two channels add rather than cancel}. \emph{But whether that channel carries what the cost is made of is a question no instrument this comparison holds can settle, and that is established rather than left open}: against two thousand draws of the covariance the channel's own cost, the part of the excess it leaves unexplained and the part it misplaces all clear, \emph{so every piece of the decomposition is acoustically modulated}---a knob moving the balance of two oscillating source terms producing an oscillating cost by construction, which is what a detection test establishes and is not what authorship needs. \emph{And the accounting that would settle it closes exactly and still cannot attribute}: the least-squares share in the plane the comb's cosine and sine span \emph{is the cosine of the phase offset identically}, so it is blind to the channel's amplitude---\emph{the anti-correlated channel being credited with essentially all of the authorship for pointing $\pi$ away}---while the one scale that is not amplitude-blind fails by measurement and carries no statistical floor, the spectra's difference moving with the data only through two fitted amplitudes. \emph{So the acoustic contrast excess is a measured discrepancy of known size, location, period and shape whose carrier is not attributable from here}, the degeneracy being exactly dimensional in that two channels span the plane; \emph{and what attribution would take is a channel independent of that plane or an estimator using amplitudes where those amplitudes carry statistical content}, neither of which is a refinement of the instruments above. \emph{And the stage at which the excess is made is located, and so now is the mechanism, which is where this sector's open work now sits}: the difference is not in the source---the
