same statistic reads $1.047$ on the projected spectrum against $0.996$ and $0.992$ on the source, and in wavenumber this arm's relative oscillation is if anything the shallower of the two---\emph{so it is made between wavenumber and multipole}, this arm retaining $1.054$ times as much of its own source oscillation as the control does and increasingly with multipole\rcpt{P15_the_acoustic_contrast_is_not_in_the_source_and_the_projection_makes_it_without_the_distance}, \emph{and not by the wavenumber sampling, the distance, the visibility width before the kernel, the lensing or the binning}, each of which is excluded by direct substitution. \emph{And the mechanism is the kernel's own acceptance in wavenumber, stated as a law rather than as an account}: for a standing oscillation the line-of-sight integral factorises with no approximation, so the comb reaches the transfer undamped and only the sum over $k$ at fixed $\ell$ can damp it, and what survives is that comb averaged over the window the sum carries---$A_{\ell}=|\!\int\!W_{\ell}e^{2ik\rs^{*}}\dd k|/\!\int\!W_{\ell}\dd k$, with no coefficient in it to fit, holding to within ten per cent of the measured amplitude on both arms at every band, and narrower here because this arm's visibility is the $14.6$ per cent wider in $\chi$ while accumulating the same sound horizon to $0.08$ per cent. \emph{What that leaves is a question about the line-of-sight integral on this background rather than about the statistics above, and it is now answered far enough to be stated as one thing rather than two.} The retention is not an artefact of how the integral is carried: the acoustic phase is already expressed in the integral's own variable, the two statements of the sound horizon agreeing identically to a part in $10^{8}$, and every other assignment the construction makes has been settled by derivation---the plasma's scales, its chemistry and the perturbations on the leaf rate, the optical depth on the stacking rate as a photon-path observable, and no free time origin. \emph{The retention is what the kernel's acceptance makes of a given projection distance, and so it turns on which rate that distance is built on}---the one assignment where what the construction specifies and what the sky prefers come apart. \emph{Run both, the specified configuration moves neither the contrast coefficient nor the residual's alternation, while carrying the projection's distance on the leaf rate removes the contrast anomaly entirely, taking it onto the value the control itself returns and bringing the acoustic scale with it.} \emph{And the comoving distance entering $j_\ell(k\chi)$ is settled, on one ground that is a theorem and a second that rests on it.} \emph{The ground is the observer's own readout}: the constant-proper-time slice is exactly flat, so the comoving angular-diameter distance \emph{is} the light-travel distance $D_C$, and on that slice the expansion law is exactly flat-$\Lambda$CDM's with radiation absent from it---a statement about the slice's induced three-metric, with no rate in it at all, which is why it can supply an identity a rule about rates cannot. \emph{The rate rule reaches the same distance by naming $D_M$, and reaches the projection's argument only through that identification}: the lapse is the rate the observable expansion rides and the shift is the projection through which it is observed as distance, so a classifier over rates fixes which rate a length accumulates on and not which length a kernel takes. \emph{And the optical depth's null-cone argument is that same rule applied a second time}, resting as it does on the optical depth being a photon-path observable of the same kind as the redshift and the distances. \emph{So the support is one established theorem rather than a count}\rcpt{P15_the_rate_rule_reaches_the_projection_only_through_the_identification_because_P7_gives_the_lapse_the_rate_and_the_shift_the_appearance_as_distance}. \emph{And a search for a second, independent support returns something better than a list of closed channels: it returns the reason there is none to find.} The one candidate that never mentions a slice is the beam side---the angular-diameter distance from the Jacobi focusing equation, whose inputs are the beam's own tidal matrix and an affine parameter. On \eqref{eq:proper-frame} that computation goes through in closed form, the metric being exactly Einstein with $\mathcal{R}=4\Lambda$ for arbitrary $M$, the areal radius an exact affine parameter on both radial null congruences, and the whole optical tidal matrix vanishing identically on them; and it returns the flat length. \emph{But the rays that carry it carry a blueshift}---$1+z$ of $0.099$ and $0.008$ between the same pair of fundamental worldlines, against the construction's own $1090.9$, wrong in sign and so beyond the reach of any tolerance. \emph{They are not the rays the construction's redshift is defined on, so the candidate is independent and is not a support.} The reason is structural: $D_M$'s two factors are read on two different objects, the comoving length a separation on the flat constant-$\tau$ slice and the redshift a ratio of the layer's own areal radii, and the slice is Riemannian and so carries no null congruence for a beam argument to be about. \emph{A second support could therefore not be an invariant computed inside either presentation; it would have to be a statement about the reassignment that joins them}, which is not a diffeomorphism\rcpt{P15_the_one_ground_admits_exactly_one_independent_second_support_and_it_is_disqualified_because_the_rays_that_carry_the_flat_length_carry_a_blueshift}.

\emph{Which settles what kind of claim the equality is, and it is not the kind a second support could strengthen.} Under the reassignment the photon congruence \emph{is} the constant-$\tau$ family (\S\ref{sec:properframe}), so the comoving length the kernel takes is a separation read on the constant-$\tau$ slice by what that congruence is, and not by an inference that something else might second or contradict. \emph{The theorem supplies the slice's metric and the reassignment supplies the slice}; together they make the identification constitutive rather than evidential. That is a narrower position than three independent routes and a firmer one: the assignment the sector's verdict rests on cannot be seconded, and equally cannot be undercut by the absence of a second support. \emph{Two readings that might have moved it are excluded rather than left open.} The construction does carry a second radial object---$r_0$, the layer's areal radius---but it is \emph{already spent}: it sets the source's discrete spectrum where $D_C$ projects, the two differing by the stretch $2.774$ the low-multipole floor is built on, and reading the angular part at face value would give $D_M=r_0$ at every redshift, which the comb excludes outright at $\pi r_0/r_s=109$ against $303$. \emph{And the proper frame's line element genuinely is not the standard comoving form}---there the radial-to-angular ratio is the Hubble radius, $r/(\mathrm dr/\mathrm d\tilde\tau)=\alpha\tanh(3c\tilde\tau/2\alpha)=c/H$ identically---\emph{but that does not move the kernel, because the standard form is claimed on the observer's constant-$\tilde\tau$ slice and not on that chart}\rcpt{P15_the_kernels_distance_is_the_observers_own_flat_slice_so_the_rate_rule_does_reach_it_and_the_disagreement_with_the_sky_is_real}\rcpt{P15_the_acoustic_contrast_is_not_in_the_source_and_the_projection_makes_it_without_the_distance}\rcpt{P15_the_accounting_closes_but_cannot_attribute_because_the_share_is_the_cosine_of_a_phase_offset}\rcpt{P15_the_null_harness_survives_its_one_assumption_except_for_the_misplaced_part_and_floors_a_phase_at_the_excess_but_not_at_the_spectrum}\rcpt{P15_all_three_projections_clear_the_instrument_noise_null_so_the_term_mixs_cost_is_combed_after_all}\rcpt{P15_the_term_mix_does_not_carry_the_modulation_and_it_survives_in_the_part_no_channel_explains}\rcpt{P15_the_resolution_table_eight_of_the_eleven_instruments_cannot_represent_the_comb_period_and_four_smallest_shares_are_honest_blanks}\rcpt{P15_the_term_mix_is_the_first_channel_to_produce_cost_where_the_arm_is_rejected_and_the_excess_there_is_combed_at_the_acoustic_period}\rcpt{P15_the_step_survives_a_differential_estimator_and_no_single_bilinear_term_carries_the_shared_step_though_the_doppler_leads_it}\rcpt{P15_the_window_free_reading_cannot_resolve_the_first_cycle_the_flatness_was_a_trend_and_nine_tenths_of_the_step_is_common_to_both_arms}\rcpt{P15_the_bank_already_existed_and_the_doppler_carries_a_growing_minority_of_the_excess}\rcpt{P15_the_retention_is_the_combs_average_over_the_kernels_own_k_acceptance_and_the_law_has_no_fitted_coefficient}\rcpt{P15_the_licensed_configuration_moves_neither_statistic_and_the_swing_was_read_above_where_it_converges};

\emph{So the comparison is not between a specified configuration and a preferred one: the specified one is the construction's, three times over, and the preferred one restores to the rate the single term this cosmology removes from it.} \emph{What this leaves is a result about this cosmology rather than a defect in its machinery}---a disagreement with the microwave sky that is localised to the acoustic contrast, quantified, and in a direction no parameter reaches. \emph{And the one step that could have replaced the identification with a derivation is not available on this construction, which is established rather than assumed.} $j_\ell(kD_C)$ is licensed by an \emph{identification}---the flatness of the distance slice together with the exact recovery of the expansion law---and the alternative would be to expand a plane wave on the proper frame's own slice with the observer at a general point of it. \emph{Carried out, that expansion contributes nothing and cannot be set up.} It contributes nothing because on a Euclidean slice the addition theorem about an off-centre observer is exact and the offset is a pure phase that cancels in $C_\ell$: no measure, no weight, no $\ell$-dependent factor, so the kernel's \emph{form} is confirmed. \emph{What it cannot do is be set up on the reassigned chart, and the reason is that the chart is the wrong presentation for it rather than that the construction lacks one.} There the cosmological surface is the $45^\circ$ line with an $S^2$ of directionality rather than the sphere (\S\ref{sec:properframe}), every epoch sitting on spheres about the branch point so that the recombination locus is at $r_0$ and not at $D_C$---short by exactly the stretch $2.774$---while that chart's own null geodesics give $1+z=(1\pm v_o)/(1\pm v_e)$, on which the construction's locus is a \emph{blueshift} and the radial area distance saturates\rcpt{P15_the_kernels_distance_is_the_observers_own_flat_slice_so_the_rate_rule_does_reach_it_and_the_disagreement_with_the_sky_is_real}. \emph{Those are not the geodesics the redshift is read on, and a mode expanded against them is not a mode of the thing observed.} \emph{And the presentation it belongs in is not simply the de~Sitter one either, because what the expansion needs is three things no one of the construction's presentations supplies together.} It needs the cosmological layer as a sphere, so that there are harmonics to expand on; it needs the lap resolved from the branch point out to the cosmological seam, since that arc is where this sector's physics sits---the acoustic epoch falling in the first part of the $120^\circ$ third that runs from $r=0$ to the handover from deceleration to acceleration at $r=\alpha/\sqrt3$; and it needs the lift carried, the stretch of the bead's path across which the areal radius climbs through $r=0$ while cosmic time does not advance. \emph{The de~Sitter presentation gives the first and buries the second}---the whole lap, seam to seam, is the hyperboloid's finite minimum equator, so the sector is compressed onto the throat---\emph{and it is excluded a second time, numerically, by what its own layer would do to the projection}. On that layer the comoving distance to last scattering is $\chi_{\rm rec}=D_C/\alpha=2.6876=0.8555\pi$, so last scattering sits $85.6$ per cent of the way to the antipode: \emph{there is no small-$\chi$ limit available, so the layer's own hyperspherical kernel is not a correction to the flat one but a different answer}, mapping through $\alpha\sin\chi_{\rm rec}=2286\ \mathrm{Mpc}$ in place of $D_C=1.4011\times10^{4}$ and placing the comb at $49.4$ against the measured $302.9$\rcpt{P15_the_kernels_distance_is_the_observers_own_flat_slice_so_the_rate_rule_does_reach_it_and_the_disagreement_with_the_sky_is_real}. \emph{And the Hopf structure does not enter the projection either, which is a measured negative and not a silence}: the degree-$L$ harmonics on the $S^3$ are the $(L/2,L/2)$ of $SU(2)\times SU(2)$ and the Hopf circle lies in one factor, so the invariant content is the other's spin-$L/2$---\emph{exactly the $S^2$ harmonics of degree $\ell=L/2$ for even $L$ and empty for odd}. That is a selection rule rather than a distance, and it would carry the lowest physical mode $L=2$ to $\ell=1$, the sky's own pure-gauge dipole, where the flat projection places it at $7.85$; \emph{so a comb of order unity is what the Hopf reading predicts, and the Hopf direction's role is the one \S\ref{sec:properframe} already gives it---traded into $\chi$ by the reassignment}. \emph{The proper $(\tau,\chi)$ chart reaches the branch point but cannot carry the lift}, which collapses there onto a single segment at vanishing cosmic time. \emph{And the presentation that does carry the whole bundle continuously across real $r$, lift included, is the bead flattened against arc length}---one curve, and so with no angular structure left to expand on. \emph{And the object that holds all three is not a fourth presentation but the harmonics' own parameter}: complex conformal time along the bead, $\dd\eta=\dd\tilde\tau/r$, the variable the mode equation is already written in. The layer is a sphere of radius $|r(\tilde\tau)|$ at every point of the curve, so $(L,M)$ is defined everywhere; every leg has finite $\eta$-length, so nothing is compressed onto the throat; and the lift's $\eta$ is \emph{purely imaginary}, a segment at right angles to the two Lorentzian legs. \emph{In it the lap closes as an exact $1:\sqrt3:2$ right triangle}---collapse $c_0B(\tfrac13,\tfrac16)/4$ real, lift $c_0B(\tfrac16,\tfrac12)/2$ imaginary, expansion $c_0B(\tfrac13,\tfrac16)/2$ real, with $c_0=2/\sqrt3\,2^{1/3}$, \emph{each leg taken whole}---the collapse horn from the far past to the comoving turnaround, the lift, and the expansion horn from the branch point outward without bound---the two ratios being identities by reflection, $2B(\tfrac16,\tfrac12)/B(\tfrac13,\tfrac16)=\Gamma(\tfrac12)^2\cdot2/\Gamma(\tfrac13)\Gamma(\tfrac23)=2\sin(\pi/3)=\sqrt3$, so that the collapse and lift sum to the expansion's modulus with argument exactly $-\pi/3$. \emph{The two numbers are the construction's own}: $\sqrt3=\alpha/r_N$, and $\pi/3$ is half the $120^\circ$ third the lap is built from~\cite{JanzenCRframework}. \emph{The closure is on those three whole legs and on no sub-stretch of them}, and the point is worth making because the lap carries a second exact factor of two that pairs different objects. The seam-bounded pieces are $1.0311\alpha$ from the back seam to the turnaround and $2.3759\alpha$ from the branch point to the front seam---neither of them its horn's length, and not in the ratio $2$. \emph{In cosmic time, though, those same two pieces stand at $0.8780\alpha$ to $0.4390\alpha$, which is exactly $2:1$}, by the identity $\operatorname{arccosh}2=2\operatorname{arcsinh}(1/\sqrt2)=\ln(2+\sqrt3)$. \emph{So one factor of two is conformal and on the whole horns and the other is in cosmic time and on the seam-bounded pieces, and neither is to be read with the other's stretches}\rcpt{P07_the_back_seam_is_the_laps_one_non_degenerate_horizon_and_its_surface_gravity_is_the_beads_own_acceleration_there}. \emph{One further use of the word names a different object again}: the ``collapse leg'' of \S\ref{sec:envelope} is the \emph{leaf} congruence's, and that one does terminate at the seam. \emph{And the question answers in two halves.} \emph{The angular label crosses untouched, exactly and without an integration}---the bead's angular block is $r^2\dd\Omega^2$, the lift moves $r$ at fixed angles, and $(L,M)$ is a discrete label on a continuous path, so it cannot change along one. \emph{The amplitude does not.} On the lift the mode equation is $u_{ss}=(k^2+a_{ss}/a)u$ with $s$ real: both signs flip together, so nothing oscillates anywhere on it, and of the two-dimensional space of mode data exactly a one-dimensional subspace reaches $r=0$ regular---\emph{which is \S\ref{sec:coherence}'s one characteristic datum per mode, obtained here as a property of the curve rather than of the surface}. On that subspace the transmitted amplitude is a pure-number function of $k$ alone: $T(0)=1$ exactly, since $u=a$ solves the lift equation identically at $k=0$, and $T(k)\to2^{7/3}k^2e^{-k\,s_{\rm tot}}$ with $s_{\rm tot}=\Gamma(\tfrac16)\sqrt\pi/\Gamma(\tfrac23)\sqrt3\,2^{1/3}=3.3387380$---\emph{the lift's own conformal length, and parameter-free: no $\Lambda$, no $H_0$, no $\Omega_m$, no mass and no epoch anywhere in exponent or prefactor}. \emph{So what the lift adds is an envelope and exactly no phase}, $\operatorname{Re}\Delta\eta=0$ identically across it rather than small. \emph{A comb is a phase, so this is not the acoustic sector's missing piece}---and what it is instead is stated in \S\ref{sec:throat}: the construction's own isotropizing step, every $L>0$ damped with not one adjustable quantity in it\rcpt{P15_the_angular_label_crosses_the_lift_untouched_and_the_amplitude_does_not_because_the_lift_is_purely_imaginary_conformal_time_so_what_it_adds_is_an_envelope_with_exactly_no_phase}. \emph{So the kernel's form rests on an expansion whose offset-observer behaviour is established, and its distance on an identification}, with the derivation that would replace the identification located rather than absent\rcpt{P15_the_harmonic_expansion_in_the_proper_frame_is_not_bounded_because_no_slicing_of_it_carries_both_the_discrete_source_and_the_projection_distance}\rcpt{P15_the_expansion_in_the_de_sitter_presentation_is_not_bounded_either_because_the_sphere_lives_at_zero_mass_and_the_observer_at_the_nariai_one_and_both_closed_kernels_are_excluded_by_the_comb}\rcpt{P15_the_licensed_configuration_moves_neither_statistic_and_the_swing_was_read_above_where_it_converges}\rcpt{P15_the_phase_is_already_carried_into_the_projections_variable_and_the_two_statements_of_the_sound_horizon_agree_identically}.
\item the exact large-angle \emph{depth} (\S\ref{sec:largescale}): the flat-projection transfer of the discrete closed-$S^3$ source is built with a \emph{genuine Boltzmann transfer}---the exact CMB temperature transfer $\Delta_\ell(k)$ (Sachs--Wolfe, early and late integrated Sachs--Wolfe, and Doppler, all common to CR and flat-$\Lambda$CDM) sampled at the discrete modes $k_L$, gate-validated (the continuum reproduces the reference $C_\ell$ to four figures) with the $r_0$ sensitivity measured rather than asserted---shape stable, depths drifting up to $15\%$ at $\ell=4$ (receipt: \texttt{verify\_lowell\_boltzmann.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_lowell_boltzmann}). 

It gives a \emph{mild} deficit, $\approx0.487$, $0.435$, $0.359$ and $0.666$ of the $\Lambda$CDM expectation at $\ell=2,3,4,5$---\emph{a dip whose minimum falls at $\ell=4$, not at the quadrupole}, the shape and the depth both reproduced by the programme's own photon hierarchy to three per cent---recovering by $\ell\approx8$: the late integrated Sachs--Wolfe term is sourced above the floor and is therefore retained by the discrete source, leaving the deficit shallower than the bare Sachs--Wolfe starvation. 

The mechanism is robust---parameter-free, located at $\ell\lesssim8$, bottoming at $\ell=4$, with $\ell=2$ and $\ell=3$ starved \emph{together} (the exact discrete measure and the Doppler term each leave the $\ell=2$-to-$\ell=3$ ratio near unity: receipts \texttt{verify\_lowell\_exact\_measure.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_lowell_exact_measure}, \texttt{verify\_doppler\_lowell.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_doppler_lowell}). 

Confronted with the sky the sector is a \emph{wash}: CR's smooth $\sim0.4$--$0.5$ deficit sits above the sharp observed quadrupole ($\sim0.2$) and below the near-$\Lambda$CDM octopole ($\sim0.6$--$1.0$), matching neither, and the exact cosmic-variance likelihood returns $\Delta(-2\ln L)=+1.58$ over $2\le\ell\le8$ and the same to the digit over $2\le\ell\le10$, both arms being at unity by $\ell=8$ (range $+0.2$ to $+3.4$ across the octopole estimator), a small residual inside cosmic variance --- \emph{and it is a near-cancellation rather than a uniform cost}: per multipole the quadrupole rewards the construction by $-2.5$ and $\ell=3$ to $5$ penalise it by $+1.0$, $+2.0$ and $+1.0$, with nothing above\rcpt{P15_the_low_multipole_depth_gap_closes_and_two_defects_were_cancelling}. So the low multipoles are a genuine but \emph{non-discriminating} sector, neither a parameter-free correspondence success nor a sharp falsification edge; the deficit's existence and location stand as a real parameter-free feature, but its depth places it consistent with $\Lambda$CDM within the lowest multipoles' cosmic variance.

\emph{The location is robust and the depth is not, and the difference between them is now measured rather
than estimated.} On the background the distance data fix, the discrete scale moves by a quarter of a per cent
and the lowest mode lands at $\ell\simeq7.8$, so \emph{where} the deficit sits is not a background choice.
\emph{The two Boltzmann treatments still differ by a factor of two at $\ell=4$} --- and that factor is not the
geometry: moving the discrete scale alone buys three per cent against a gap of a hundred. \emph{It is the late integrated Sachs--Wolfe term}, which at these multipoles is sourced above the discrete
floor and is therefore retained by the sum, filling the deficit; the treatment that truncated its
line-of-sight integration earlier was deeper at exactly the multipoles where the two disagreed. \emph{Carried
to the present instead, the two treatments agree}: on the exact transfer the deficits are $0.487$, $0.435$,
$0.359$, $0.666$, $0.911$, $0.983$ and $0.998$ of the flat expectation at $\ell=2$ through $8$ ---
\emph{the exact transfer being the one carried here, with the hierarchy agreeing} to within three per cent at
every multipole and one per cent above $\ell=5$\rcpt{P15_the_low_multipole_floor_moves_with_no_background_and_the_factor_two_is_the_late_isw}\rcpt{P15_the_low_multipole_depth_gap_closes_and_two_defects_were_cancelling}. \emph{The factor of two was the missing term and
not a difference between the constructions}, and the agreement is stable: it does not move under a doubled
opacity grid, a halved late step count, or a further-extended continuum. \emph{So the shape and the depth
both cross-validate} --- minimum at $\ell=4$, recovery by $\ell=8$ --- and the sector is ready to be scored
against the measured multipoles with cosmic variance carried. 

The exposed edges lie elsewhere---the acoustic contrast difference of the first item and the damping signature it carries;
\item the progenitor spectrum itself---$n_s$ and $A_s$ (\S\ref{sec:transmission})---inherited boundary data fixed by the progenitor collapse, the baryogenesis-analogue computation belonging to the matter sector~\cite{JanzenOperator,JanzenRange}, with the target a consistency between the inherited composition and spectrum and the measured primordial abundances and tilt, not a derivation from the geometry alone---the most ambitious reach of the four, the one that would close the decomposition from both ends and give CR a derivation where the standard model keeps a measured datum. \emph{And the target is now a number rather than a direction}: refitting both arms on this construction's own background prefers a tilt of $0.995$ here against $0.956$ on the standard one (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound})\rcpt{P15_the_route_from_the_handover_amplitude_to_the_spectral_parameters_is_already_in_print_as_the_hbar_sentence_so_the_tilt_passes_exactly_and_horn_two_cannot_carry_it_at_all}, \emph{so a derivation has to land near the bluer value and not near the one the standard model infers}. \emph{And what supplies it is the progenitor's vacuum and not the collapse leg}: on the leg both the potential and the effective temperature are functions of the single variable $k\eta/\sqrt3$, so a handover at a fixed phase is exactly scale-free---the ratio of amplitudes at two wavenumbers is one---and a handover at a fixed time would contribute not a tilt but a running, red in both channels and growing as $k^{2}$, which no amplitude choice makes look like a constant. \emph{At the branch point it vanishes quadratically}, so the leg is a wavenumber-independent amplitude and
nothing else. \emph{And what the substrate alone fixes is $n_s=1$ exactly}: in conformal time the mode
equation carries no length, so the curvature radius enters only as the prefactor that sets the amplitude and
reaches the tilt for no value of it; and on the forced member the progenitor's mass is that same radius up to
a pure number, so the two candidate scales are one and fall together. \emph{But the fluctuations are not
generated on the substrate}, and the interior they are generated in carries a scale the substrate does not.
\emph{Solved there}, the mode potential interpolates between the scale-free matter form and a radiation form
that is not, so the generated spectrum \emph{breaks} at the wavenumber the radiation content sets: its
log-slope runs from $+0.30$ to $+1.98$ across the observed band, tending to the radiation value $+2$.
\emph{So it is a running and not a tilt}, by the same band test that closes the collapse leg --- and
\emph{blue at every wavenumber}, where the measured value is red by two parts in a
thousand\rcpt{P15_the_interiors_vacuum_is_blue_everywhere_and_a_running_so_the_substrates_mass_template_does_not_apply}. \emph{The flattest slope goes as the
radiation fraction}, so exact scale-invariance is the pure-dust limit and reaching red would need that
fraction negative. \emph{And the running is the interior's own and not the vacuum's, which is what the two readings of this
interior share.} Measured on unit incoming amplitude, so that what comes out is the multiplier itself, the
transfer's log-slope runs from $-0.866$ near the bottom of the observed band to $-0.010$ near its top: it is
\emph{neither scale-free nor a power law}, and what it adds to any spectrum's tilt is twice that spread.
\emph{The exact scale-invariance the vacuum shows is that same function's low-wavenumber limit}: below the
break the log-slope tends to $-1$ and $kT(k)$ to a constant, and the vacuum's $k^{3}|T|^{2}$ is
scale-invariant exactly where that slope is $-1$. \emph{So the cancellation is the vacuum normalisation
meeting the transfer's low-wavenumber power law, and it holds only where that power law does.} The split is
an identity rather than an argument---the tilt is $\dd\ln T/\dd\ln k+1$, the wavenumber dependence always
the transfer's and the constant always the normalisation's. \emph{And the transfer is the same function for
every input}, linear to parts in $10^{14}$ and reproducing its slope to parts in $10^{12}$ across inputs
including one that is not a power law\rcpt{P15_the_interiors_transfer_imprints_its_own_running_so_the_channel_hunt_answered_the_wrong_question}.
\emph{So the question is not what the progenitor's vacuum gives but what this interior does to whatever
enters it}, and the answer is that \emph{no power-law progenitor spectrum emerges with a red, near-constant
tilt}: an input that did would have to run by $1.71$ to $1.72$ across the band, opposite in sense to the
transfer, the spread being the differencing convention rather than the measurement, and the figure moving
by two per cent over the range the arm's own distance mapping allows.
\emph{That is a requirement on the progenitor and not a result about it}, and it is stated as one.
\emph{And it is bounded, because the transfer's running is a function of one variable.} In the interior's
own radial variable the potential carries no radiation fraction at all---the fraction and the wavenumber
enter only through their product---so the log-slope is a universal function of that product and the break
sits at a fixed value of it, which places the break wavenumber inversely with the radiation fraction
exactly rather than approximately. \emph{At the other end the potential is Coulombic}: the crunch's $1/x$
against the mode's $k^{2}$ is the repulsive zero-angular-momentum Coulomb wave equation, and the expansion
of its irregular solution's amplitude carries the log-slope to zero as $1/k$. \emph{So the excursion is
exactly one, from $-1$ to $0$; what the transfer can add to any tilt is at most two; and no band, however
wide, can ask a progenitor for a running above it}---the observed band asking $86$ per cent of that
maximum, which is a structural statement and not an empirical one. \emph{The limit is derived where the
integration is not trusted and only checked where it is}: past the wavenumber at which the incoming mode
would start inside the radiation era rather than outside the break the construction's premise fails, and
the asymptote is compared with the solved transfer only below that wavenumber, where the two agree to
better than two per cent. \emph{And what forces the requirement is the radiation fraction.} A fraction
that carries the whole observed band below the break---where the transfer is a clean power law and a
power-law progenitor would come through untilted---does exist, below $10^{-3}$ on the looser of two stated
criteria for where the break is and below $1.4\times10^{-4}$ on the stricter; but it lies fifty-five to
nearly four hundred times under the determined value, so the requirement stands because the composition
this construction determines elsewhere forces it. \emph{Both runnings are then set by the same object},
the break, so a progenitor that cancels this one is not a tuning of an arbitrary function but a progenitor
whose own spectrum breaks where the interior's does---a structural candidate, and nothing here shows that
any progenitor
does\rcpt{P15_the_transfers_running_is_a_function_of_one_variable_so_the_requirement_is_the_radiation_fractions}.
\emph{And the substrate's power-law formula does not apply off the substrate}: its
coefficient is dimensionless, which is what makes it a power law, where the interior's carries a scale---a distinction invisible to a tilt read off a background fitted to the other arm. \emph{The figure is provisional in one respect and it is stated}: it is measured on the range below the damping tail, which is where a tilt has most of its leverage. \emph{One route to it is closed, and closing it narrows the
target rather than the ambition.} The construction is recursive---each progenitor seeds the next
cosmology---so a \emph{stationary} recursion would make $A_s$ a fixed point and determine it. But entropy per
baryon rises strictly at every branch-point crossing, the crossing being an erasure and the increment bounded
below by Landauer's $k_B\ln2$ independently of the network and of $\eta$~\cite{JanzenCosmogenesis}: \emph{a map
that strictly increases a coordinate has no fixed point}, and independently the chain it generates is finite, so
there is no asymptotic limit for a fixed point to be the limit
of\rcpt{P15_the_fixed_point_route_is_closed_by_the_monotone}. \emph{So the derivation must reach the head of a
bounded genealogy rather than a stationary state}---the same terminus the inherited composition reaches, and the
two are one question asked of two quantities;
\item the \emph{likelihood-level model selection}: the parameter-free ratio comparison above is joined by the exact low-multipole cosmic-variance likelihood---each $\hat C_\ell$ a scaled $\chi^2_{2\ell+1}$ about the model $C_\ell$, the absolute baseline cancelling in the CR/$\Lambda$CDM ratio (\texttt{verify\_lowell\_likelihood\_v2.py}\rcpt{P15_verify_lowell_likelihood_v2}), the proper instrument where cosmic variance dominates. 

With the genuine-Boltzmann depths ($\approx0.49/0.44$ at $\ell=2/3$, above) the sector is a \emph{wash}: the low quadrupole still mildly rewards CR ($\Delta(-2\ln L)\approx-2.5$, its milder dip nearer the observed low power than $\Lambda$CDM's), the octopole and $\ell=4$ mildly penalise it ($\approx+1.0$ and $+2.0$)---\emph{so the sector's largest single-multipole penalty sits not at the octopole but at $\ell=4$, the prediction's own minimum}---and the net over $2\le\ell\le10$ is $\Delta(-2\ln L)\approx+1.6$ (range $+0.2$ to $+3.4$ across the octopole estimator)---a small residual well inside cosmic variance. A Monte-Carlo over $10^4$ skies puts the sector's \emph{in-principle} discriminating power at only $\sim3.4\sigma$ (the short multipole lever arm and cosmic variance), and the current measurement uncertainty swamps even that. 

The low multipoles are therefore a genuine but \emph{non-discriminating} test: CR and $\Lambda$CDM are statistically indistinguishable there, the mild deficit neither favouring CR nor refuting it. Decisiveness comes from outside the sector---the geometric expansion rate~\eqref{eq:native-rate}, confronted with the distance ladder (\S\ref{sec:tensions}), as the standing discriminator, and the likelihood across the \emph{full} spectrum, which is run and rejects this arm's spectrum on $TT$ shape (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound}), its amplitude level not established by the time-reversal driving equality (\S\ref{sec:coherence}).
\end{enumerate}
A frontier of a different kind concerns the matter itself rather than the perturbation observables above: the \emph{dynamics of matter crossing the branch point}. 

Three facts about that crossing follow from the structure already established here. 

It is \emph{well posed}---the beginning is fixed at the branch point $r=0$, and the crossing is taken on the \emph{substrate}, whose curvature is finite there however the geometry's own invariants behave. 

The bend $\rho=m'/4\pi r^{2}$ that carries the matter is accordingly finite, and its data on that null surface is characteristic rather than Cauchy: a characteristic crossing with no obstruction from the substrate's curvature. \emph{The geometry's Kretschmann scalar does diverge at that locus}, as the abstract states and the circle paper computes~\cite{JanzenCircle}; what makes the crossing well posed is that the divergence belongs to the chart-borne geometry and not to the substrate the cut is taken on, and that the tortoise measure converges so the crossing carries no scale. 

It \emph{isotropizes and is scale-free}---the throat's de~Sitter no-hair (\S\ref{sec:throat}) damps the anisotropic part of the crossing stress-energy to the isotropic monopole, and the degenerate approach (\S\ref{sec:transmission}) imprints no thermal scale: the matter counterparts of the field no-hair and faithful transmission established above. \emph{Where each of those acts is worth stating, since one of them cannot act at the crossing at all.} 

Damping is a process and requires elapsed time, and the crossing has none: the real part of the complex cosmic time is frozen along the segment reaching it~\cite{JanzenCanonicalTime}. 

The no-hair damping therefore belongs to the \emph{approach}---the throat region, over the collapse leg's own cosmic time---while the crossing carries whatever survives it unchanged, not because a mechanism protects it but because there is no interval in which anything could act. \emph{The isotropy is earned on the approach and preserved by the crossing}, and the two halves are of different kinds: the first is a damping calculation with a rate, a timescale and a residual, the second follows from the zero-duration structure and takes no model input. It follows that \emph{how much anisotropy survives is fixed by the approach against the leg's duration}---a property of the progenitor's collapse history rather than of the crossing. And it obeys a \emph{structural transition law}: because the reassignment preserves the cosmic foliation and the density is leaf-carried and lapse-independent~\cite{JanzenOperator}, the reassignment acts on the time-stacking---fixing the $\Lambda$-set rate~\eqref{eq:rate}---and not on the leaf, so the isotropic density crosses as inherited progenitor content, which is the structural reason the inherited datum of \S\ref{sec:tensions} is inherited rather than produced; \emph{and two conditions must be met at that crossing, which a concrete model has to satisfy separately: the reassignment requires a degenerating Killing field whose freed null direction can be re-founded as a clock, the joining of the legs requires a branch point, and these are independent---the forced Nariai member carries the degeneracy and is not a branch point, while $r=0$ is the branch point and carries no Killing degeneracy~\cite{JanzenDynamics}, so a model furnishing one without the other furnishes no cosmogenesis.} \emph{The first of the two is settled as a uniqueness, not merely an availability}: across the slicing family the reassignment that promotes a null direction to the fundamental congruence is \emph{uniquely} the one selecting the forced Nariai member, every other vantage yielding a congruence that crosses the embedding's symmetry structure transversally and so reading as a localised black hole~\cite{JanzenGroupoid}. \emph{A model therefore has no freedom in where the reassignment acts}, and misplacing it does not fail to give a cosmology by a little---it gives a black hole instead. The scoping of the two rates this law forces---the excursion-side local readout against the observable geometric rate, their boundary---is drawn out in the cosmogenesis paper~\cite{JanzenCosmogenesis}. What remains open is the \emph{detailed} worldline and field dynamics of the crossing for a concrete matter model~\cite{JanzenCRframework,JanzenOperator}---a depth left for a matter model to supply, and resting on a crossing shown well posed rather
than merely postponed.

None of these unsettles the result obtained: the branch point's null geometry decomposes the primordial scalar spectrum into a substrate-determined structure and a progenitor-supplied content, and the branch point transmits that content rather than imprinting one of its own; the super-horizon transfer across the branch point is itself
computed---$\Phi\to\Phi_i$ for every $k$, with the expanding leg inheriting
$\tfrac{9}{10}\Phi_i$ scale-invariantly\rcpt{C21_the_superhorizon_transfer_closes}---and carrying the acoustic evolution from that
datum through to recombination is the transfer of \S\ref{sec:refit-bound}, which is run and returns the comparison recorded there. They are, rather, the map of where this cosmology goes next---what displaces the fourth peak, and the full oscillating medium downstream in the matter sector, the tensor sector's companion in the dynamics paper~\cite{JanzenDynamics}.

\section{Discussion}\label{sec:discussion}

What this paper determines is one thing seen from several sides. A single de~Sitter geometry, its scale set by the cosmological constant alone (the maximally symmetric substrate's sole scale~\cite{JanzenGeometricCore}) and read through a causal reassignment at the cosmogenesis branch point, \emph{is} the observed cosmology: the flat-$\Lambda$CDM expansion history is its areal radius (\S\ref{sec:flatlcdm}); the acoustic scale is computed from its geometric rate, which the distance data and that angle fix together (\S\ref{sec:tensions}); the primordial scalar spectrum is the characteristic data of its branch point, structure fixed by the substrate and content inherited across the branch point (\S\S\ref{sec:coherence}--\ref{sec:transmission}). The Einstein field equations are unchanged; what changes is the reading, and under it the quantities the standard model carries as independent inputs---the present matter density, the dark-energy fraction---are readings of one cosmic clock, not drivers of the expansion, so that the coincidence of $\Omega_{m}$ and $\Omega_\Lambda$ dissolves into our observing at a time of order the geometry's one timescale. There is a single seed, and the observed universe is what grows from it. And the one scale reaches down as well as out: the same cosmological constant that sets this expansion sets, for every mass $M$, the \emph{Hubble--Eddington radius}~\cite{Eddington1933} $r_{\mathrm{HE}}=(3GM/\Lambda c^{2})^{1/3}$---the boundary within which local structure stays bound against the cosmic flow and beyond which it is carried off. The slicing geometry reads that boundary as the flat locus of the existent slice's own curvature, the local bend of the cut exactly cancelling the substrate's cosmological term~\cite{JanzenSlicing}, and the slicing operator reads it as the handover from sub-marginal bound orbits to the marginally-bound $E=1$ congruence that \emph{is} this cosmology~\cite{JanzenOperator}. So the substrate's single $\Lambda$ governs both the global expansion this paper computes and the local scale at which structure resists it---one constant read at two ranges---and the Hubble--Eddington radius, proposed as a local, per-structure test of $\Lambda$ from the largest bound structures~\cite{PavlidouTomaras2014}, is an independent observational handle on the very constant the expansion history here measures. The two ranges are moreover the same length up to a pure number. The Hubble--Eddington radius---where the
slicing surface's Gaussian curvature $K_{G}=1/\alpha^{2}-M/r^{3}$ vanishes, the local bend cancelling the
substrate's cosmological curvature~\cite{JanzenSlicing}---is $r_{\mathrm{HE}}=(M\alpha^{2})^{1/3}$; while the
expansion's own amplitude, $(2M\alpha^{2})^{1/3}$, is exactly the areal radius at matter--$\Lambda$ equality
($\rho_m/\rho_\Lambda=\operatorname{csch}^{2}(3\tilde\tau/2\alpha)$, unity at $\sinh=1$). The two therefore
stand in the fixed ratio $2^{1/3}$ for every $M$: the scale at which a bound structure's gravity balances the
expansion and the scale at which the expansion's matter balances its $\Lambda$ are one length read locally and
cosmologically, the factor of two that of the Schwarzschild term against the surface curvature's $M/r^{3}$ and
nothing else. \rcpt{P15_two_ranges_ratio}

The comparison with the standard cosmological model is accordingly not \emph{only} a contest of fits---though the geometric rate fits the joint acoustic-and-distance-ladder data at one $H_0$ (\S\ref{sec:tensions})---but also, and more deeply, a difference in what each must assume to do so. The standard model \emph{assembles} the observed universe: a dark-energy component for the late-time acceleration, an inflationary sector for the causal contact, flatness, coherence, and near-scale-invariance the background dynamics do not themselves supply, and further early-universe physics where the sound horizon and the expansion rate come into tension. Each part is a real solution to a real problem, and each was introduced to yield the feature it produces. \emph{This cosmology adds no such part.} Causal contact, isotropy, flatness, phase coherence, and scale-free transmission are read off structure the construction already contains---the isotropizing throat (\S\ref{sec:throat}), the null boundary on which coherence \emph{is} the characteristic-data condition (\S\ref{sec:coherence}), the exactly Euclidean constant-time slice (\S\ref{sec:largescale}), the scale-free crossing that transmits rather than imprints (\S\ref{sec:transmission})---each fixed by $\Lambda$ alone, with no parameter free to produce it. This is the distinction between a structure that \emph{requires} a phenomenon and one that merely \emph{permits} it through adjustable apparatus~\cite{JanzenShadowExistence,JanzenModernParallax}, and it is the criterion by which competing frameworks are rightly weighed ahead of the measurement that will decide between them.

\emph{The dark-energy component is the plainest case of the difference}: on this cosmology the acceleration is not a sector at all. Since $(\mathrm{d}r/\mathrm{d}\tilde\tau)^{2}=E^{2}-f$ on the energy family, one has $\mathrm{d}^{2}r/\mathrm{d}\tilde\tau^{2}=-f'/2=rK_{G}$: \emph{the comoving acceleration is the existent slice's own Gaussian curvature}, up to the positive factor $r$.\rcpt{P03_acceleration_is_slice_curvature} So the expansion decelerates while the slice is negatively curved, turns over exactly where it is flat---the Hubble--Eddington radius, $\rho_{m}=2\rho_{\Lambda}$, at $\sinh(3c\tilde\tau/2\alpha)=1/\sqrt2$---and accelerates thereafter, with nothing added to make it do so.  \emph{And that locus is a
measurement, not only a description}: the turnover sits where the areal radius reaches the front seam,
$r=r_{N}$, so the acceleration-onset redshift is the offset parameter itself minus one,
\begin{equation}\label{eq:zacc}
z_{\mathrm{acc}} \;=\; x_{0}-1 \;=\; 0.6648 \pm 0.0467 ,
\end{equation}
against $\bigl(2\Omega_\Lambda/\Omega_m\bigr)^{1/3}-1=0.632$ on Planck's parameters---the two agreeing to
well within the quoted interval, and with no fit performed here. \emph{That interval is carried as an input
throughout rather than derived here}, so it is quoted as the stated uncertainty on the offset parameter and
not converted into a significance.  \emph{What makes the equation worth stating is what it does
not contain}: $z_{\mathrm{acc}}$ is a redshift, measured calibration-free, so it is untouched by the
distance-ladder tension, and it fixes $x_{0}$ without $H_{0}$, without a ladder and without the microwave
background.  The single dimensionful scale is where any disagreement must then live, and the age follows
from the same parameter in closed form, $t_{0}=(2r_{N}/\sqrt3\,c)\operatorname{asinh}(x_{0}^{3/2}/\sqrt2)$. The late-time acceleration is thus read off the same structure that fixes the local boundary of a bound system.

\emph{And the growth sector, written on this rate, loses its parameters entirely --- which turns a known
numerical curiosity into a statement about the epoch.} Substituting $H=(c/\alpha)\coth u$ and the Friedmann
readout's $4\pi G\rho=\tfrac32(c/\alpha)^{2}\operatorname{csch}^{2}u$ into the growth equation and changing to
$u$, every factor of $(c/\alpha)^{2}$ divides out:
\begin{equation}\label{eq:growth-native}
3D''+4\coth(u)\,D'-2\operatorname{csch}^{2}(u)\,D=0 .
\end{equation}
\emph{The scale does not merely fail to appear---it cancels}, because at Nariai the source is fixed by
$\Lambda$ and the time variable carries $\alpha$ too. \emph{The decaying mode is the rate itself},
$D_{-}=\coth u=H\alpha/c$, and reduction of order gives
$D_{+}=\coth u\int_{0}^{u}\sinh^{2/3}v\,\operatorname{sech}^{2}v\,\dd v$. \emph{And the matter fraction is a
clock reading}: $\cosh^{2}u=1/\Omega_m$ exactly, so $\Omega_m=\operatorname{sech}^{2}u$.

\emph{On these variables the normalisation of the standard growth factor} ---
$J(\Omega_m)=\int_{0}^{\infty}(1+z)\,\dd z/[\Omega_m(1+z)^{3}+1-\Omega_m]^{3/2}$, \emph{known to cross unity
once near the concordance density}~\cite{JanzenGrowthNorm} --- \emph{becomes
$J=\tfrac23\cosh^{2}u_{0}\,(D_{+}/a)|_{0}$, whose matter-domination limit
$D_{+}/a\to\tfrac35$ returns the analytic endpoint $J\to\tfrac25$.} Differentiating the condition $J=1$ gives
an exact identity between the two sides' derivatives, $\propto\tfrac92\Omega_m-2$, and integrating it from the
common origin leaves
\begin{equation}\label{eq:moment-balance}
\int_{0}^{u_{0}} a(v)\,\Omega_m(v)\bigl[\Omega_m(v)-\tfrac23\bigr]\,\dd v \;=\; 0 ,
\end{equation}
\emph{the growth-weighted history of $\Omega_m$ balanced about $\tfrac23$}\rcpt{P15_the_growth_root_is_a_moment_balance_pivoting_on_the_nariai_radius}.

\emph{The pivot is the deceleration-to-acceleration turnover}: $\Omega_m=\tfrac23$ is
$\rho_m/\rho_\Lambda=2$ exactly. \eqref{eq:moment-balance} is solved at $\Omega_m=0.31516$, so \emph{the
density at which that normalisation equals unity is fixed by an equation with nothing free in it}.

\emph{Two scope statements belong with that, and both narrow it.} \emph{The turnover is not this
construction's property}: any flat matter-and-$\Lambda$ rate has it, and what is this construction's is the
\emph{identification} of that turnover with a geometric locus --- the areal radius there being
$\alpha/\sqrt3=r_{N}$, the Nariai radius and the merged double root (above). \emph{So
the balance pivots on the turnover, and the geometry is what makes the turnover a place.}

\emph{And the rate used is the stacking one, which is the right rate for the object but not for this
construction's own growth.} The published normalisation is a matter-and-$\Lambda$ integral, and the stacking
rate written in the fitted pair \emph{is} that rate, which is why the derivation reproduces it. \emph{The rate
rule assigns perturbations to the leaf} (\S\ref{sec:properframe}), and on the leaf rate the same integral
returns $0.99940$ rather than unity. \emph{So what is derived here is the root of the standard
normalisation}, and what remains contingent is that the present epoch lies near it.

\emph{What that figure does \textbf{not} measure is how far the root moves, and saying so matters because it
invites that reading.} Continuing the closed form onto the leaf rate is a closed form applied to a rate it
does not solve. \emph{Solved directly, the root moves the other way and by less} --- the leaf root sitting at
$u_0=1.180039$ against the stacking $1.180309$, a shift of $1.1\%$ of the gap to the measured epoch and
\emph{away} from it, taking the offset from $0.704$ to
$0.713\sigma$\rcpt{P15_the_growth_root_on_the_leaf_rate_is_not_a_moment_balance_and_the_gap_stands}. \emph{So
the root's two-per-cent offset from the measured epoch---$x_{0}=1.6315$ at the leaf root against the measured
$1.6648$---is not an artefact of the rate}: it stands, and wants a mechanism.

The transmission dichotomy (\S\ref{sec:transmission}) is where the distinction between requiring and permitting is sharpest, because there the two frameworks make opposite structural commitments about one object. A non-degenerate horizon's exponential approach imprints a scale-invariant spectrum---the very mechanism by which inflation produces near-scale-invariance---while the degenerate Nariai seam carries no scale and the branch point, where the modes cross, accumulates no phase at all, so the progenitor spectrum is transmitted unaltered. Inflation accommodates the observed tilt because a freely specified potential can be tuned to it; reading that fit as a confirmation mistakes a model with enough adjustable structure to match for one that required what was found (\S\ref{sec:predictions}). This cosmology cannot tune: the finite tortoise coordinate at the branch point \emph{requires} that the crossing not imprint a scale, so the framework has no inflationary scale-invariant attractor, no consistency relation, and no substrate-sourced primordial $B$-modes. The amplitude and tilt it does not derive it inherits, exactly as flat $\Lambda$CDM inherits the baryon-to-photon ratio from a baryogenesis it does not model; on that single datum, of the same kind and count the standard model already carries, the two stand level. A framework is credited for requiring the structure it explains, not faulted for measuring the boundary data it does not---and the asymmetry falls on everything else.

The verdict this licenses must be drawn on the right axis, and confined to it. On the axis of theory-choice---the requiring rather than the permitting of the phenomena, the consolidation of many results under one structure, the absence of a device added per problem---the comparison is decidable from the structural record, and it falls to this cosmology~\cite{JanzenShadowExistence}. On the axis of data-discrimination the picture is split: the geometric rate fits the acoustic scale and the baryon-acoustic distance ladder together at one $H_0$ (\S\ref{sec:tensions}), leaving the discrepancy with the local ladder where the standard model leaves it---while on the light elements and the low multipoles the two frameworks are even. \emph{On the microwave-background peak structure they are not}: the transfer's likelihood comparison rejects this arm's spectrum on TT shape (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound}), and that is the sharpest datum against the construction that the corpus holds. So the standing is stated as it stands: favoured on the structure, level with the standard model on the light elements and the low multipoles, and carrying a spectrum computed from a background fitted to the distances alone---which reproduces the comb to a part in six hundred and is rejected on shape. The data axis is not even, and where it is uneven it runs against the construction, which is the direction a framework that has staked falsifiable commitments---no scale-invariant attractor \emph{reaching the observer}, no consistency relation, no substrate $B$-modes---must be prepared for, precisely where the standard model keeps its freedom\rcpt{P15_the_continuation_is_diagonal}.

The low-multipole deficit is a mild, non-discriminating feature (\S\S\ref{sec:largescale}, \ref{sec:scope}). The flat projection of the discrete closed-$S^3$ source gives a parameter-free deficit below $\ell\approx8$---real, and set by $\Lambda$ through $r_0$ and $D_C$---bottoming at $\ell=4$, at $\approx0.49$, $0.44$, $0.36$ and $0.67$ of the $\Lambda$CDM expectation at $\ell=2,3,4,5$. At that depth CR's smooth deficit matches neither the sharp observed quadrupole-only dip nor the near-$\Lambda$CDM octopole, and under the exact cosmic-variance likelihood the two frameworks are statistically indistinguishable at low $\ell$ ($\Delta(-2\ln L)\approx+1.6$ over $2\le\ell\le10$, inside cosmic variance). So the sector neither confirms nor refutes: it is a genuine parameter-free prediction whose depth places it consistent with $\Lambda$CDM within the lowest multipoles' cosmic variance. The framework's exposed edges are the acoustic peak structure, where the transfer reproduces the comb and differs from the sky in the acoustic contrast (\S\ref{sec:refit-bound}), and the damping-\emph{scale} signature (\S\ref{sec:coherence}), a computed, non-reabsorbable effect whose size the handover fixes and whose sign the diffusion integral's endpoint does. Its sharpest test is the geometric expansion rate itself, carried to the distance ladder and confronted with the DESI~DR2 baryon-acoustic dataset at $\chi^2/\text{dof}\simeq1$ (\S\ref{sec:tensions}): the rate that carries the full ladder is the rate the spectrum is computed on, so the two are one commitment.

The framework's standing includes what the standard model has genuinely built and this cosmology has not. Dark matter is inferred from multiple independent, cross-consistent lines---rotation curves, gravitational lensing, the merger offsets, the acoustic-peak structure, the growth of structure---and this cosmology carries the matter density as the bend of the spatial cut without yet removing the component or answering its particle question; that column stands at full weight. Standard nucleosynthesis derives the light-element abundances from one measured parameter, a genuine achievement this cosmology meets on the same footing rather than exceeds: the collapse's cooling leg is a standard nucleosynthesis run on one inherited datum (\S\ref{sec:tensions}; the cosmogenesis paper~\cite{JanzenCosmogenesis}), producing helium-4 and deuterium at their observed values---the freeze-out temperature the data demands being the bottleneck the standard rate delivers---and, because the run is standard, carrying lithium-7's standard threefold over-prediction, the lithium problem shared rather than solved. On the light elements the two frameworks stand even: the same two successes, the same one problem, one progenitor handover fitting the pattern together, and this cosmology's open edge the last-percent precision against the measured abundances. And the framework is young: the \emph{detailed} dynamics of matter crossing the branch point (its crossing shown well posed and structurally governed, \S\ref{sec:scope}), the derivation---as against the measurement---of the inherited data, and the Standard-Model matter \emph{content}---the gauge representations and the mass spectrum, which the framework does not supply, its geometry shown to force only the discrete flavour skeleton~\cite{JanzenMatter}---remain to be built (\S\ref{sec:scope};~\cite{JanzenCRframework}). These are open frontiers, not defects: a theory is not marked down for what it has not built, only for a claim it makes that fails. They are named plainly here because an economy that favours this cosmology on the structure it carries would be worth nothing bought by concealing the structure it does not.

\section{Conclusion}\label{sec:conclusion}

So, plainly, what this paper claims and what it does not. It claims that the observed expansion history, the computation of the acoustic scale, and the structure of the primordial scalar spectrum are consequences of a single de~Sitter geometry read through the causal reassignment its own horizon geometry selects---one seed, grown, the field equations unchanged---and that on the economy of assumption, the criterion by which theory-choice is rightly made ahead of the deciding measurement, this reading is favoured over the assembled standard picture. On the data axis it claims a more limited thing: that the axis is split---the expansion history fitting the distance ladder, which fixes $H_0$ with the acoustic angle, and the abundances confirmed, while the acoustic peak structure, computed from that same background and scored by the full-spectrum likelihood, counts against it---with that structure and the damping-tail signature the edges still to be settled (the low multipoles a mild non-discriminating deficit consistent with $\Lambda$CDM within cosmic variance). It does not claim to be a complete cosmology: its matter sector, the derivation of its progenitor spectrum, and its fermion content remain to be built. It claims to have shown that the universe we observe is the reading of one geometry, and to have staked the falsifiable commitments by which that claim can be tried.

A final word on the register, because it is the thesis rather than an aside. The beginning this cosmology describes is the branch point at $r=0$---the locus at which the collapse leg of
the cosmogenetic contour becomes the expansion leg~\cite{JanzenCRframework}---and it is not a curvature
singularity reached and left behind. \emph{The curvature there is set by the substrate's own scale.} The
throat is $\alpha$, the defining constant of the maximally symmetric manifold the cut is taken on, and the
divergence of the invariants computed in the areal chart is that chart degenerating, exactly as a polar chart
degenerates at a pole---not a curvature scale of the geometry~\cite{JanzenCosmogenesis, JanzenGeometricCore}.
Two further facts stand with it: no finite cosmic-time layer ever reaches the locus, and the continuation
through it carries no scale, since $f\to-2M/r$ diverges so that the tortoise coordinate
$r_{*}=\int\dd r/f$ \emph{converges} and what is transmitted is fixed by the analytic structure rather than by
a length. \emph{The branch point is crossed by continuation, not traversed by a worldline.} The expansion is not a space ejected from a point but the reading, on the near side of that branch point, of a spatial layer still advancing, the branch point itself a collapse that in the frame it bounds is never complete. The distinction the whole programme turns on---between what has finished happening and what is still underway---is thus the distinction the cosmology is built from: the universe is not the collapsed record of a beginning but the still-evolving layer that record represents, read here as one de~Sitter geometry.
